3. In the case of complicity, the act of the principal is considered.

###### Artigo 112º

Suspension of limitation

1. The limitation of criminal proceedings is suspended, in addition to cases specially provided for by law, during the time when:

- a) The proceedings cannot legally commence or continue due to lack of legal authorization or a judgment to be delivered by a non-criminal court, or by reason of the referral of a preliminary question to a non-criminal court;
- b) The offender serves, abroad, a custodial sentence or security measure;
- c) The criminal proceedings are pending, from the moment the accused is notified of the charge.
  - 2. The limitation period resumes running from the day on which the cause of suspension ceases.
  - 3. The cause of suspension cannot, depending on the situations, exceed half of the period provided for in Article 110.

##### CAPÍTULO II

LIMITATION OF SENTENCES AND SECURITY

MEASURES

###### Artigo 113º

Limitation periods for sentences

1. Sentences are subject to limitation in the following periods:

- a) 25 years if they exceed 12 years of imprisonment;
- b) 20 years if they exceed 8 years but do not exceed 12 years of imprisonment;
- c) 12 years if they exceed 4 years but do not exceed 8 years of imprisonment;
- d) 8 years in all other cases of imprisonment;
- e) 4 years in the case of fines.
  - 2. The limitation period for sentences is counted from the final judgment of the decision imposing it.

###### Artigo 114º

Limitation of accessory sentences

The limitation of accessory sentences is subject to the regime of the principal sentence.

###### Artigo 115º

Limitation periods for security measures

Security measures are subject to limitation in the following cases:

- a) 15 years if custodial;
- b) 5 years if non-custodial;
- c) 2 years in cases of revocation of firearms license.

###### Artigo 116º

Suspension of limitation

1. The limitation of sentences and security measures is suspended, in addition to cases specially provided for by law, during the time when:

- a) By force of law the execution cannot commence or continue;
- b) After the escape of the convicted person and until recaptured;
- c) The convicted person is serving another custodial sentence or security measure;
- d) The deferral of payment of the fine continues;
- e) The convicted person is temporarily prevented from performing community service;
- f) The execution is taking place.
  - 2. The limitation period resumes running from the day on which the cause of suspension ceases.
  - 3. The provisions of paragraph 3 of Article 112 apply correspondingly.

##### CAPÍTULO III

NON-LIMITATION

###### Artigo 117º

Crimes of genocide, against peace and humanity and war crimes

Criminal proceedings and sentences imposed for crimes of genocide, against peace and humanity and war crimes are not subject to limitation.

##### CAPÍTULO IV

OTHER CAUSES OF EXTINCTION

###### Artigo 118º

Other causes

In addition to cases specially provided for by law, criminal liability is extinguished by the death of the offender, by amnesty and by pardon.

###### Artigo 119º

Death of the offender

The death of the offender extinguishes the criminal proceedings, as well as the criminal sanction imposed on him.

###### Artigo 120º

Amnesty

Amnesty extinguishes criminal proceedings and terminates the execution of the sanction not yet fully or partially served, as well as its effects and accessory sentences insofar as possible.

###### Artigo 121º

Amnesty and concurrence of crimes

Unless otherwise provided, amnesty is applicable to each of the crimes that constitute the concurrence.

###### Artigo 122.º

Pardon

Pardon extinguishes the sentence, in whole or in part, or substitutes it with another provided for by law and more favorable to the convicted person.

## LIVRO II

SPECIAL PART

#### TÍTULO I

OF CRIMES AGAINST PEACE AND HUMANITY

##### CAPÍTULO I

CRIMES OF GENOCIDE AND AGAINST

HUMANITY

###### Artigo 123º

Genocide

1. Whoever, with intent to destroy, in whole or in part, a national, ethnic, racial or religious group, commits:

- a) Homicide or serious physical or mental harm to members of the group;
- b) Acts that by any means prevent procreation or the birth of members of the group;
- c) Rape, sexual slavery, enforced prostitution, forced pregnancy, enforced sterilization or any other form of sexual violence of comparable gravity;
- d) Forcible transfer of members of the group to another group;
- e) Acts that by violent means prevent the group from settling or remaining in geographic areas traditionally or historically recognized as theirs;
- f) Subjecting the group to conditions of existence or to cruel, degrading or inhuman treatment likely to bring about its total or partial destruction;
- g) Confiscation or widespread seizure of property belonging to members of the group;
- h) Prohibition of certain commercial, industrial or professional activities to members of the group;
- i) Spread of epidemic likely to cause death or physical harm to members of the group;
- j) Prohibition, omission or prevention by any means of providing members of the group with humanitarian assistance adequate to combat situations of epidemic or severe food shortage; is punishable by 15 to 30 years of imprisonment.
  - 2. Whoever publicly and directly incites the commission of genocide is punishable by 5 to 15 years of imprisonment.

###### Artigo 124º

Crimes contra a humanidade

Whoever, within the framework of a widespread or systematic attack against any civilian population, commits acts from which result:

- a) Homicide or serious physical or mental injury;
- b) Extermination, understood as the subjection of all or part of the population to adverse living conditions, such as deprivation of access to food or medicine, suitable to cause the death of one or more persons;
- c) Enslavement;
- d) Deportation or forced transfer of a population, understood as the unlawful displacement of one or more persons to another State or location through their expulsion or other coercive act;
- e) Imprisonment or any other severe form of deprivation of physical liberty of a person, in violation of the rules or principles of international law;
- f) Torture, understood as the act consisting of inflicting severe physical or mental pain or suffering on a person deprived of liberty or under the control of the agent;
- g) Rape, sexual slavery, forced prostitution, forced pregnancy, forced sterilization or any other form of sexual violence of comparable gravity;
- h) Persecution, understood as the deprivation of the enjoyment of fundamental rights, in violation of international law, to a group or collectivity that can be identified on political, racial, national, ethnic, cultural, religious, gender grounds or on the basis of other grounds universally recognized as unacceptable in international law;
- i) Enforced disappearance of persons, understood as the arrest, detention or abduction promoted by a State or political organization, or with its authorization, support or acquiescence, followed by a refusal to acknowledge such state of deprivation of liberty or to provide any information on the situation or location of those persons, with the purpose of denying them the protection of the law for a prolonged period of time;
- j) Apartheid, understood as any inhuman act committed in the context of an institutionalized regime of systematic oppression and domination by one racial group over another racial group or groups, with the intention of maintaining that regime;
- k) Other inhuman acts of a similar character intentionally causing great suffering, serious injury or affecting mental or physical health; is punishable by imprisonment from 15 to 30 years.

##### CAPÍTULO II

WAR CRIMES

###### Artigo 125º

War crimes against persons

1. Whoever, within the framework of an international armed conflict or non-international armed conflict, against a person protected by international humanitarian law, commits:

- a) Homicide;
- b) Torture or cruel, degrading or inhuman treatment including biological experiments;
- c) Serious physical injury or acts causing great suffering;
- d) Hostage-taking;
- e) Compelling to serve in enemy armed forces or proceeding to recruit or enlist children under 18 years of age into armed forces, military or paramilitary forces of a State, or into armed groups distinct from the armed forces, military or paramilitary forces of a State, or their use to participate in hostilities;
- f) Severe, prolonged and unjustified restrictions on the liberty of persons;
- g) Deportation or transfer, or unlawful deprivation of liberty;
- h) Unjustified misappropriation or destruction of property of great value;
- i) Conviction and execution of sentence, without prior fair and impartial trial;
- j) Acts that outrage the dignity of the human person, in particular through humiliating and degrading treatment;
- k) Homicide or injuries inflicted on a combatant who has laid down arms or who, having no means to defend himself, has unconditionally surrendered or in any way placed himself out of combat;
- l) The acts described in paragraph g) of the previous article;
- m) Subjecting persons who are under the control of a belligerent party to physical mutilations or to any type of medical or scientific experiments that are not motivated by medical, dental or hospital treatment, nor carried out in the interest of those persons, and that cause death or seriously endanger their health; is punishable by imprisonment from 12 to 25 years.
  - 2. The penalty is increased by one-fifth in its limits when the acts referred to in the preceding paragraph are committed against members of a humanitarian institution.
  - 3. Whoever, within the framework of an international armed conflict:
- a) Transfer, directly or indirectly, as an occupying power, part of its own civilian population into the occupied territory or transfer all or part of the population of the occupied territory, within or outside that territory;
- b) Compel a prisoner of war or other protected person to serve in the armed forces of an enemy power;
- c) After the cessation of hostilities, delay, without justified reason, the repatriation of prisoners of war; is punishable by imprisonment from 15 to 30 years.

###### Artigo 126º

War crimes by use of prohibited methods of warfare

Whoever, within the framework of an international armed conflict or non-international armed conflict:

- a) Attack the civilian population in general or civilians not taking direct part in hostilities;
- b) Attack civilian objects, that is, objects that are not military objectives;
- c) Attack, by any means, population centers, dwellings or buildings that are not defended and that are not military objectives;
- d) Launch an indiscriminate attack affecting the civilian population or civilian objects, knowing that such attack causes loss of human life, injury to civilian persons or damage to civilian objects, which are excessive;
- e) Use the presence of civilians or other protected persons to prevent certain points, areas or military forces from being the object of military operations;
- f) Deliberately cause starvation of the civilian population as a method of warfare, depriving it of goods indispensable to its survival;
- g) Declare or threaten, as an officer, that no quarter will be given;
- h) Kill or injure enemy combatants by treachery;
- i) Launch an attack, being able to know that it causes widespread, long-term and severe damage to the environment which are clearly excessive in relation to the concrete and direct overall military advantage anticipated;
- j) Commit perfidy, understood as the act of killing, injuring or capturing, which appeals, with intent to deceive, to the good faith of an adversary to make him believe that he has the right to receive, or the obligation to ensure the protection provided by the rules of international humanitarian law; is punishable by imprisonment from 15 to 30 years.

###### Artigo 127º

War crimes by use of prohibited means of warfare

1. Whoever, in the context of an international armed conflict or a non-international armed conflict, employs weapons, projectiles, materials and methods of combat that, by their very nature, cause superfluous injury or unnecessary suffering or have indiscriminate effects, in violation of international law applicable to armed conflicts, is punished with imprisonment from 12 to 25 years.

2. The previous number includes, namely, the use of:

- a) Poison or poisoned weapons;
- b) Asphyxiating, toxic or similar gases or any similar liquid, material or device;
- c) Bullets that expand or flatten easily inside the human body, such as hard-jacket bullets that do not completely cover the interior or have incisions;
- d) Anti-personnel mines;
- e) Chemical weapons;
- f) Weapons whose main effect is to injure with fragments not detectable by X-rays in the human body;
- g) Incendiary weapons;
- h) Laser weapons that cause blindness.
  - 3. The weapons, instruments and products referred to in the previous number are those considered as such by international law.

###### Artigo 128º

War crimes against property protected by distinctive signs or emblems

Whoever, in the context of an international armed conflict or a non-international armed conflict, attacks:

- a) Personnel, installations, material, units or vehicles participating in a peacekeeping or humanitarian assistance mission, in accordance with the United Nations Charter, provided that they are entitled to the protection conferred by international humanitarian law on civilians or civilian property;
- b) Buildings, installations, material, units or vehicles, duly marked with the distinctive emblems of the Geneva Conventions or personnel authorized to use the same emblems. is punished with imprisonment from 10 to 20 years.

###### Artigo 129º

War crimes against property

Whoever, in the context of an international armed conflict or in the context of a non-international armed conflict:

- a) Misappropriates, destroys or damages property on a large scale or of great value, without military necessity or in an illegal and arbitrary manner;
- b) Attacks, destroys or damages buildings dedicated to religious worship, education, arts, sciences or charity, cultural or historical monuments, archaeological sites, hospitals and places where sick and wounded are gathered, provided they are not military objectives;
- c) Pillages a city or town, even when taken by assault; is punished with imprisonment from 5 to 15 years.

###### Artigo 130º

War crimes against other rights

Whoever, in the context of an international armed conflict or in the context of a non-international armed conflict, declares abolished, suspended or inadmissible in court, any rights and procedures of nationals of the enemy party is punished with imprisonment from 5 to 15 years.

##### CAPÍTULO III

AGAINST PEACE AND FREEDOM

###### Artigo 131º

Terrorist organizations

1. A terrorist group, organization or association is considered to be a grouping of two or more persons who, in order to achieve political, ideological, philosophical or religious purposes, act in concert, aiming to harm national integrity or independence, prevent, alter or subvert the functioning of national or international institutions, intimidate or force public authorities, international organizations or certain persons, groups of persons or the general population, to perform an act, to refrain from performing it or to tolerate it being performed, through the commission of serious crimes:

- a) Against the life, physical integrity or freedom of persons;
- b) Against the security of transport and communications, including telegraphic, telephone, radio or television;
- c) Of intentional production of common danger, through fire, explosion, release of radioactive substances or toxic or asphyxiating gases, flood or avalanche, collapse of construction, contamination of food and water intended for human consumption or spread of disease, plague, harmful plant or animal;
- d) That involve the use of nuclear energy, firearms, explosive substances or devices, incendiary means of any nature, booby-trapped parcels or letters.
- e) Acts that destroy or make it impossible to function or divert from their normal purposes, definitively or temporarily, in whole or in part, means or routes of communication, public service installations or international installations or installations intended for the supply and satisfaction of the vital needs of the population.
- f) Research and development of biological or chemical weapons
  - 2. Whoever promotes or founds a terrorist group, organization or association, joins, participates in or supports them, is punished with imprisonment from 12 to 25 years.
  - 3. Whoever leads or directs a terrorist group, organization or association is punished with imprisonment from 15 to 30 years.
  - 4. When a terrorist group, organization or association, or the persons referred to in numbers 2 or 3, possess any of the means indicated in paragraph d) of number 1, the penalty is aggravated by one third in its minimum and maximum limits.
  - 5. Preparatory acts for the establishment of a terrorist group, organization or association are punished with the penalty reduced to half in its minimum and maximum limits.
  - 6. The penalties referred to may be extraordinarily mitigated or no punishment may take place if the agent voluntarily abandons his activity, prevents or seriously attempts to prevent the danger caused by it or the continuation of terrorist groups, organizations or associations, or communicates their existence to the authority so that it can prevent the commission of crimes.

###### Artigo 132º

Terrorism

1. Whoever commits any of the crimes provided for in paragraphs a) to c) and e) of number 1 of the previous article, or any crime with the use of the means referred to in paragraphs d) or f) of the same provision, with the intention referred to therein, is punished with imprisonment from 12 to 25 years, or with the penalty corresponding to the crime committed, aggravated by one third in its minimum and maximum limits, if the penalty is equal to or greater.

2. The penalty may be extraordinarily mitigated or no punishment may take place if the agent voluntarily abandons his activity, removes or considerably reduces the danger caused by it, prevents the result that the law seeks to avoid from occurring, or concretely assists in the collection of decisive evidence for the identification or capture of other perpetrators.

###### Artigo 133º

Financing of terrorism

Whoever by any means, directly or indirectly and intentionally, provides, collects or holds funds or property of any type, as well as products or rights capable of being transformed into funds, attempts to do so, with the intention that they be used or knowing that they may be used, in whole or in part, in the planning, preparation or for the commission of the acts provided for in number 1 of article 131º, or commits these acts with the intention referred to in number 1 of article 132º, is punished with imprisonment from 12 to 25 years.

###### Artigo 134º

Incitement to war

1. Whoever, by any means, publicly and repeatedly, incites hatred against a race, a people or a nation, with the intention of provoking a war or preventing peaceful coexistence between different races, peoples or nations, is punished with imprisonment from 2 to 8 years.

2. Whoever entices or recruits Timorese citizens or foreigners to, in the service of a foreign group or power, wage war against a State or to overthrow the legitimate Government of another State by violent means, is punished with imprisonment from 5 to 15 years.

###### Artigo 135º

Racial or religious discrimination

1. Whoever founds, constitutes an organization or develops organized propaganda activities that incite or encourage racial or religious discrimination, hatred or violence, as well as whoever participates or provides assistance, including its financing, to the organization or the aforementioned activities, shall be punished with imprisonment from 4 to 12 years.

2. Whoever, in a public meeting, through writing intended for dissemination or through any means of social communication, disseminates ideas with the intention of inciting racial or religious discrimination or encouraging it, or provoking acts of violence against a person or group of persons because of their race, color, ethnic origin or religion, shall be punished with imprisonment from 2 to 8 years.

##### CAPÍTULO IV

COMMON PROVISIONS

###### Artigo 136º

Responsibility of military commanders and other superiors

1. The military commander or person acting as such who, having knowledge that forces under their effective command and control or under their effective responsibility and control are committing any of the crimes provided for in this title, does not adopt all necessary and adequate measures to prevent or repress their commission or to bring it to the immediate knowledge of the competent authorities, shall be punished with the penalty corresponding to the crime or crimes that are effectively committed.

2. The provisions of the previous paragraph apply, with due adaptations, to the superior regarding the control of subordinates under their effective authority and control.

###### Artigo 137º

Definitions

For the purposes of this Title, the following are considered:

- a) "Armed conflict of an international character", one that:
- i) Occurs between States, even without a formal declaration of war, even if the state of war is not recognized by one of them;
  - ii) Corresponds to a situation of total or partial occupation of the territory of a State, even if that occupation does not encounter any military resistance;
  - iii) Falls within a situation in which peoples fight against colonial domination, foreign occupation and against segregation regimes, in the exercise of the right of peoples to self-determination, enshrined in the Charter of the United Nations and in the declaration concerning the principles of international law with respect to friendly relations and cooperation among States;
- b) "Armed conflict of a non-international character", one that takes place in the territory of a State, is of a prolonged nature and opposes governmental authorities and organized armed groups or these among themselves, with the exception of situations of internal disturbances and tensions, such as sporadic or isolated acts of violence or others of a similar nature;
- c) "Protected persons":
- i) In international armed conflicts, the persons protected for the purposes of the Geneva Conventions of 1949 and Additional Protocol I, namely the wounded, sick, shipwrecked, prisoners of war, medical or religious personnel and civilian population;
  - ii) In armed conflict of a non-international character, the wounded, the sick, the shipwrecked, as well as persons not taking active part in hostilities in the power of the enemy;
  - iii) In armed conflict of an international character and in armed conflict of a non-international character, members of the armed forces and combatants of the enemy party who have laid down their arms or have no other means of defense.

#### TÍTULO II

CRIMES AGAINST PERSONS

##### CAPÍTULO I

AGAINST LIFE

###### Artigo 138º

Simple homicide

Whoever kills another person shall be punished with imprisonment from

8 to 20 years.

###### Artigo 139º

Aggravated homicide

If death is produced in circumstances that reveal special culpability or perversity, namely:

- a) With the use of poison, torture, asphyxiation, fire, explosive or other insidious means or that results in the commission of a crime of common danger, or also, with another act of cruelty to increase the suffering of the victim;
- b) Through concealment or other means or resource that makes defense on the part of the victim difficult or impossible;
- c) Out of greed, for the pleasure of killing, for excitement or to satisfy sexual instinct, through payment or reward or its promise, or for any other futile or base motive;
- d) With the purpose of preparing, executing or covering up another crime, facilitating the escape or ensuring the impunity of the perpetrator of a crime;
- e) Out of racial, religious or political hatred;
- f) With premeditation, this being understood as coldness of mind, reflection on the means employed or postponement of the intention to kill for more than 24 hours;
- g) If the victim is a spouse, descendant, ascendant, collateral, related family member up to the second degree, adoptee of the agent or person who lives with them in analogous conditions where there is a hierarchical, economic or work dependency;
- h) If the victim is a particularly vulnerable person due to age, illness or physical or mental disability;
- i) If the victim is a member of a sovereign body or constitutional political bodies, member of a local administration body, magistrate, defender, lawyer, court officer, civil servant or any other person in charge of a public service, provided that it is in the exercise or because of the exercise of their functions;
- j) If the victim is a witness, declarant, expert, injured party or offended party and the crime is committed with the purpose of preventing testimony, the reporting of facts or the filing of a complaint or because of their intervention in the process. the agent shall be punished with imprisonment from 12 to 25 years.

###### Artigo 140º

Negligent homicide

1. Whoever, through negligence, kills another person, shall be punished with imprisonment up to 4 years or a fine.

2. In cases where the agent has acted with gross negligence, they shall be punished with imprisonment up to 5 years.

###### Article 141º*

Interruption of pregnancy

1. Whoever, by any means and without the consent of the pregnant woman, causes her to abort is punished with a prison sentence of 2 to 8 years.

2. Whoever, by any means and with the consent of the pregnant woman, causes her to abort is punished with a prison sentence of up to 3 years.

3. The pregnant woman who gives consent to the abortion performed by a third party, or who, by her own act or that of another, causes herself to abort, is punished with a prison sentence of up to 3 years.

4. The provisions of the previous paragraphs do not apply when the interruption of pregnancy constitutes, in accordance with the state of medical knowledge and experience, and after all possible actions have been carried out to save the life of the pregnant woman and that of the fetus or embryo, the only means to remove danger of death to the pregnant woman, provided it is performed, with medical certification, by a doctor or other health professional under their direction in a public or officially recognized health facility, and with the consent of the pregnant woman.

5. The verification of the circumstances referred to in the previous paragraph is certified in a medical certificate written and signed by a panel of three doctors, on a date prior to that of the interruption, of which the doctor who performs or supervises the performance of the interruption of pregnancy is not a part.

6. Consent is given in writing in a document signed by the pregnant woman – after hearing, if possible, the spouse or the person who lives with her under conditions analogous to those of spouses – or by another person on her behalf, whenever possible in advance of two days in relation to the date of the interruption.

7. In the case of the pregnant woman being a minor, consent is given by the legal representative.

8. In the case of the pregnant woman being of legal age or emancipated but psychically incapable, consent is given, respectively and successively, by the spouse or the person who lives with her under conditions analogous to those of spouses, by the legal representative, by an ascendant or descendant, or, in their absence, by any relatives of the collateral line.

9. When it is not possible to obtain the medical certificate referred to in paragraph 5, and/or it is not possible to obtain consent in accordance with paragraphs 6 to 8, and the performance of the interruption is urgent and cannot be postponed, the doctor decides in conscience according to the situation, resorting, whenever possible, to the opinion of other doctors.

10. The panel referred to in paragraph 5 consists of doctors possessing adequate knowledge for the assessment of the circumstances that justify the interruption of pregnancy, including, whenever possible, a gynecologist/obstetrician.

11. The doctors who issue the medical certificate and the doctors who perform or supervise the performance of the interruption of pregnancy must provide all necessary clarifications to the pregnant woman and, if applicable, to the persons referred to in paragraphs 7 and 8, namely the interruption method to be used, the effects of the interruption, the possible consequences for the physical and psychological health of the pregnant woman.

12. Doctors and other health professionals are recognized the right to conscientious objection with respect to any acts concerning the interruption of pregnancy.

13. Doctors and other health professionals who invoke conscientious objection must guarantee immediate intervention in the necessary acts, as well as the immediate care of the pregnant woman, by another health professional.

14. Conscientious objection is expressed and communicated, through a written and signed document, to the clinical head of the health facility where the objector provides service.

* Wording introduced by law 6/2009, of 15 July The previous wording of article 141º was as follows:

###### Article 141º

Interruption of pregnancy

1. Whoever, by any means and without the consent of the pregnant woman, causes her to abort is punished with a prison sentence of 2 to 8 years.

2. Whoever, by any means and with the consent of the pregnant woman, causes her to abort is punished with a prison sentence of up to 3 years.

3. The pregnant woman who gives consent to the abortion performed by a third party, or who, by her own act or that of another, causes herself to abort, is punished with a prison sentence of up to 3 years.

4. The provisions of the previous paragraphs do not apply to cases in which the interruption of pregnancy constitutes the only means to remove danger of death or serious and irreversible injury to the body or to the physical or psychological health of the pregnant woman or the fetus, provided it is performed, with authorization and supervision of a medical panel, by a doctor or health professional in a public health facility and with the consent of the pregnant woman and or the spouse.

5. The provisions of paragraph 4 of this article are subject to autonomous legislation.

###### Article 142º

Infanticide

A mother who kills her child during childbirth or shortly thereafter and still under its disturbing influence is punished with a prison sentence of 3 to 10 years.

###### Article 143º

Exposure or abandonment

1. Whoever, intentionally, places the life of another person in danger:

- a) By exposing them in a place that subjects them to a situation from which they alone cannot defend themselves; or
- b) By abandoning them without defense, by reason of age, physical disability or illness, whenever the agent has the duty to guard, watch over or assist them; is punished with a prison sentence of 1 to 6 years
  - 2. If the act results in:
- a) A serious offense to physical integrity, the agent is punished with a prison sentence of 2 to 8 years;
- b) Death, the agent is punished with a prison sentence of 5 to 15 years.
  - 3. If the victim is the spouse, descendant, ascendant, collateral relative, relative by affinity up to the second degree, adopter or adoptee of the agent or person who lives with them under conditions analogous to those of spouses, the penalties referred to in the previous paragraphs are increased by one third in their limits.

###### Article 144º

Incitement or assistance to suicide

1. Whoever incites another person to commit suicide, or provides them with assistance for that purpose, if the suicide is actually attempted or consummated, is punished with a prison sentence of up to 3 years or a fine.

2. If the acts described in the previous paragraph are directed at any of the persons referred to in paragraph 3 of the previous article or a minor under 17 years of age or a person whose capacity for assessment or determination is significantly diminished, the penalty is imprisonment of up to 5 years.

3. Whoever, in any form and repeatedly engages in public propaganda of suicide, is punished with a prison sentence of up to 2 years or a fine.

##### CHAPTER II

AGAINST PHYSICAL INTEGRITY

###### Article 145º

Simple offenses to physical integrity

1. Whoever offends the body or health of another person is punished with a prison sentence of up to 3 years or a fine.

2. Criminal proceedings depend on a complaint.

###### Article 146º

Serious offenses to physical integrity

Whoever offends the body or health of another person with the purpose of:

- a) Depriving them of an important organ or limb;
- b) Seriously and permanently disfiguring them;
- c) Affecting the capacity to work, intellectual capacities, or capacity to procreate in a serious and lasting manner or permanently;
- d) Causing permanent illness or incurable psychological anomaly; or
- e) Creating danger to life; is punished with a prison sentence of 2 to 8 years.

###### Article 147º

Aggravation

1. Whoever, intending only to offend the body or health of another person:

- a) Causes the offenses provided for in article 146º is punished with a prison sentence of up to 5 years;
- b) Causes death by negligence, is punished with a prison sentence of 1 to 6 years.
  - 2. Whoever, intending to cause any of the offenses provided for in

###### article 146º, by negligence causes death is

punished with a prison sentence of 4 to 12 years.

3. If the crimes referred to in the two previous articles have as victims any of the persons mentioned in subparagraph i) of article 139º, because of or in the exercise of the functions listed, the penalty shall be increased by one third in its maximum and minimum limits, if a more severe punishment is not appropriate by virtue of another legal provision.

###### Artigo 148º

Negligent offenses to physical integrity

1. Whoever, through negligence, offends the body or health of another person, is punished with imprisonment up to 1 year or a fine.

2. If the negligence is gross, the offender is punished with imprisonment up to 2 years or a fine.

3. If the act results in serious bodily harm, the offender is punished with imprisonment up to 3 years or a fine.

4. Criminal proceedings depend on a complaint.

###### Artigo 149º

Medical-surgical interventions and treatments

1. Interventions and other treatments that, according to the state of medical knowledge and experience, are indicated and are carried out, in accordance with the leges artis, by a doctor or other person legally authorized to undertake them with the intention of preventing, diagnosing, combating or alleviating a disease, suffering, bodily injury or fatigue, or a mental disorder, are not considered bodily offenses.

2. If the violation of the leges artis results in danger to the patient's body, health or life, the offender shall be punished with imprisonment up to 3 years or a fine.

3. Criminal proceedings depend on a complaint.

###### Artigo 150º

Offenses by means of poisonous substances

1. Whoever offends the body or health of another by administering poisonous substances or substances harmful to physical or mental health is punished with imprisonment up to 5 years.

2. If the act results in any of the consequences provided for in Article 146 or the death of the victim, the offender is punished, respectively, with imprisonment from 2 to 6 years and from 4 to 12 years.

###### Artigo 151º

Reciprocal bodily offenses

1. When two persons offend each other, reciprocally, in body or health, neither of them acting in self-defense and none of the effects provided for in

###### artigo 146º nor the death of any of the parties occurring, they are

punished with imprisonment up to 2 years or a fine.

2. Criminal proceedings depend on a complaint.

###### Artigo 152º

Participation in affray

1. Whoever intervenes or takes part in an affray involving two or more persons, from which death or serious bodily harm results, is punished with imprisonment up to 3 years or a fine if those effects cannot be intentionally imputed to them.

2. Participation in an affray is not punishable when it is determined by a non-censurable motive, namely if it aims to react against an attack, defend another person or separate the combatants.

###### Artigo 153º

Ill-treatment of an incapacitated person

1. Whoever has under their guardianship or care, or under their responsibility for education, or as a subordinate in work activity, an incapacitated person, particularly vulnerable due to illness, advanced age, pregnancy, physical or mental disability, and causes them offenses to the body or health, or inflicts physical or psychological ill-treatment, or cruel treatment, is punished with imprisonment from 2 to 6 years, if a more serious penalty is not applicable by virtue of another legal provision.

2. If the victim is a descendant, collateral, relative or affine up to the second degree, adoptee of the offender or person who lives with them in analogous conditions, the penalty referred to in the previous number is increased by one third in its limits.

###### Artigo 154º

Ill-treatment of a spouse

Whoever inflicts physical or psychological ill-treatment or cruel treatment on their spouse or on a person with whom they cohabit in a situation analogous to that of spouses is punished with applicable by another legal provision.

###### Artigo 155º

Ill-treatment of a minor

1. Whoever, having in their guardianship or care a minor under 17 years of age, is responsible for their education or has them as a subordinate in work activity, and:

- a) Causes offenses to the body or health, inflicts physical or psychological ill-treatment, or cruel treatment
- b) Subjects them to economic exploitation, dangerous work or work capable of compromising their education or physical, mental, spiritual, moral or social development;
- c) Subjects them to any form of slavery or analogous practice;
- d) Uses, recruits or offers them for purposes of prostitution, production of pornographic material or pornographic shows; or
- e) Uses, recruits or offers them for the practice of illicit acts or activities, namely for the production and trafficking of narcotics as defined by international conventions; is punished with imprisonment from 2 to 6 years, if a more serious penalty is not applicable by virtue of another legal provision.
  - 2. Whoever, in the same situation, uses the minor to beg, is punished with imprisonment up to 3 years, if a more serious penalty is not applicable by virtue of another legal provision.
  - 3. If the victim is a descendant, collateral, relative or affine up to the second degree, adoptee of the offender or person who lives with them in analogous conditions, the penalties referred to in the previous numbers are increased by one third in their limits.

###### Artigo 156º

Aggravation by result

If, as a consequence of the ill-treatment described in Articles 153 to 155, the effects referred to in Article 146 occur, the offender is punished with imprisonment from 3 to 10 years and if death is caused the penalty is 5 to 15 years of imprisonment.

##### CAPÍTULO III

AGAINST PERSONAL FREEDOM

###### SECÇÃO I

PERSONAL ASSAULTS

###### Artigo 157º

Threats

1. Whoever, by any means, threatens another person with the commission of a crime in such a way as to cause them fear or unease or to prejudice their freedom of determination, is punished with imprisonment up to 1 year or a fine.

2. Criminal proceedings depend on a complaint.

###### Artigo 158º

Coercion

1. Whoever, by means of violence or threat of serious harm, compels another person to an action or omission, or to endure an activity, is punished with imprisonment up to 2 years or a fine.

2. Criminal proceedings depend on a complaint.

###### Artigo 159º

Serious Coercion

If the coercion is carried out:

- a) By means of the threat of a crime punishable with imprisonment exceeding 3 years,
- b) By an official seriously abusing their functions,
- c) Against a particularly defenseless person, due to age, disability, illness or pregnancy
- d) Against any of the persons referred to in paragraph i) of Article 139, the offender is punished with imprisonment up to 3 years or a fine.

###### Artigo 160º

Kidnapping

1. Whoever, outside the cases provided for in the criminal procedural law, detains, imprisons, keeps imprisoned or detained another person, or in any other way deprives them of freedom, is punished with imprisonment up to 3 years or a fine.

2. The penalty of imprisonment is from 2 to 8 years if the deprivation of freedom:

- a) Lasts more than seventy-two hours;
- b) Is carried out by means of offense to physical integrity, torture or any other cruel, degrading or inhuman treatment;
- c) Causes, through negligence of the offender, the death of the victim, or results in the victim's suicide;
- d) Has as victim someone who exercises public, religious or political authority.
- e) Is promoted, authorized or supported by a public authority agent or member of a political organization.

###### Artigo 161º

Abduction

1. Whoever, by means of violence, threat or deceit, transfers another person from one place to another with the intention of:

- a) Subjecting the victim to extortion;
- b) Committing a crime of assault, exploitation or sexual abuse;
- c) Obtaining ransom or reward; or
- d) Compelling public authority or a third party to perform an action or omission, or to endure an activity; is punished with imprisonment from 4 to 12 years.
  - 2. The applicable penalty is 5 to 15 years of imprisonment if any of the circumstances provided for in number 2 of Article 160 is verified.

###### Artigo 162º

Escravidão

1. Whoever, by any means, places another human being in a situation of slavery and uses them in that condition, is punished with imprisonment from 8 to 20 years.

2. The consent of the victim is irrelevant if any of the means referred to in the following article have been used.

3. For purposes of application of the provisions of this article, the state of submission of a person is considered a situation of slavery, even if de facto, to powers corresponding to those of a property right, or any real right, or bound to the destination of a thing.

###### Artigo 163º

Trafficking in persons

1. Whoever recruits, alienates, transfers, acquires, transports, transfers, houses or receives persons, by means of threat, use of force or other forms of coercion, abduction, fraud, deception, abuse of authority or of a position of vulnerability, or through the giving or receiving of payments or benefits to obtain the consent of a person having authority over another, for the purpose of exploitation, is punished with imprisonment from 8 to 20 years.

2. Whoever recruits, transports, transfers, houses or receives a minor under 17 years of age for purposes of exploitation, even if it does not involve any of the means referred to in the previous number, incurs the penalty provided for in the previous number.

3. For purposes of application of the provisions of this article, exploitation shall include, at least, the exploitation of the prostitution of others or other forms of sexual exploitation, forced labour or services, slavery or practices similar to slavery, servitude or the removal of organs.

4. The consent of the victim is irrelevant if any of the means referred to in number 1 have been used.

###### Artigo 164º

Aggravation

If the acts described in articles 162º and 163º are committed:

- a) As a way to facilitate the exploitation or sexual use of the victim, by the agent or by a third party;
- b) Where the victim is under 17 years of age;
- c) Where the victim is in a foreign country or moving them there;
- d) Using the victim, against their will, in the commission of crimes; or
- e) Where the agent performs an activity that confers upon them public or religious authority before a group, region or the entire country; the agent is punished with imprisonment from 12 to 25 years.

###### Artigo 165º

Trafficking in human organs

1. Whoever obtains, alienates, transfers, acquires, transports or transfers tissues, organs, substances or parts of the human body of a third party, without consent or by means of threat, use of force or other forms of coercion, abduction, fraud, deception, abuse of authority or of a position of vulnerability, or through the giving or receiving of payments or benefits, or assists in their obtaining, transaction, transport or storage, is punished with imprisonment from 3 to 10 years.

2. If from the commission of the acts referred to in the previous number results any of the consequences provided for in article 146º or the death of the victim, the agent is punished with imprisonment from 4 to 12 years and from 5 to 20 years, respectively.

3. The consent of the victim is criminally irrelevant if any of the means referred to in number 1 have been used.

###### Artigo 166º

Sale of persons

1. Whoever, outside the situations provided for in article 163º, by any act or other form of transaction, transfers a person or group of persons to another person or group of persons by payment of any amount or other consideration, reward or advantage, is punished with imprisonment from 2 to 8 years;

2. If the acts referred to in the previous number are committed:

- a) In relation to a minor under 17 years of age
- b) Abusing authority resulting from a family relationship, guardianship or curatorship, hierarchical, economic or work dependence;
- c) Taking advantage of the functions or position that, under any title, they exercise in a prison establishment, educational or correctional establishment, hospital, hospice, asylum, clinic or other health establishment or other establishment intended for assistance or treatment; or
- d) Against a person who is unconscious or incapable, particularly vulnerable by reason of illness, physical or mental disability the agent is punished with imprisonment from 4 to 12 years.
  - 3. It is criminally irrelevant the consent of the person themselves or of a third party who exercises any form of authority over the victim.

###### Artigo 167º

Torture or other cruel, degrading or inhuman treatment

1. Whoever, having as their function the prevention, investigation, decision, regarding any type of offence, the execution of respective sanctions or the protection, custody, surveillance or accompaniment of a person detained or imprisoned and tortures or treats in a cruel, degrading or inhuman manner, in order to:

- a) Obtain from them or from another person a confession, testimony, statement or information
- b) Punish them for an act committed or allegedly committed by them or by another,
- c) Intimidate them or to intimidate another person, is punished with imprisonment from 2 to 8 years.
  - 2. Also incurs the penalty provided for in the previous number whoever, on their own initiative, by order of a superior or in agreement with the competent entity to exercise the functions referred to in the previous number, assumes de facto the performance of those functions committing any of the acts described therein.
  - 3. Torture, cruel, degrading or inhuman treatment is considered to be the act that consists of inflicting acute physical or psychological suffering, severe physical or psychological exhaustion or the use of chemicals, drugs or other means, natural or artificial, with the intention of disturbing the decision-making capacity or free expression of will of the victim.

###### Artigo 168º

Aggravation

1. Whoever, under the terms and conditions described in the previous article:

- a) Produces serious bodily harm, under the terms provided for in article 146º;
- b) Employs particularly serious means or methods of torture, namely beating, electroshock, mock execution, hallucinogenic substances, sexual abuse or serious threat against family members;
- c) Commits such acts as a way to prevent or hinder the free exercise of political or associative rights constitutionally enshrined;
- d) Habitually commits the acts referred to therein; is punished with imprisonment from 5 to 15 years.
  - 2. If from the acts referred to in this article or in the previous article results suicide or death of the victim, the agent is punished with imprisonment from 5 to 20 years.

###### Artigo 169º

Failure to report

1. The hierarchical superior who, having knowledge of the commission, by a subordinate, of any of the acts described in articles 167º and 168º, does not make the report within three days immediately following knowledge thereof, is punished with imprisonment from 1 to 6 years.

2. Anyone to whom, for professional reasons and officially is given knowledge of the commission of acts described in articles 167º and 168º and does not immediately communicate to the hierarchical superior or make the respective report, is punished with the penalty set in the previous number extraordinarily mitigated.

###### Artigo 170º

Freedom of assembly or demonstration

1. Whoever interferes in an assembly or demonstration in a public place or open to the public, legally authorized, preventing or attempting to prevent its holding is punished with imprisonment up to 2 years or a fine.

2. The agent of police authority who prevents or attempts to prevent, outside the legal conditions, the exercise of the right of assembly or demonstration described in the previous number, is punished with imprisonment up to 3 years.

###### SECÇÃO II

SEXUAL ASSAULTS

###### Artigo 171º

Sexual coercion

Whoever, by means of violence, serious threat, or after, for that purpose, having rendered them unconscious or placed them in the impossibility of resisting, forces another person to suffer or to commit, with them or with another, a sexual act of relevance is punished with imprisonment from 2 to 8 years.

###### Artigo 172º

Violation

Whoever, by the means referred to in the previous article, has with another person vaginal intercourse, anal intercourse or oral intercourse or forces them to endure the introduction of objects into the anus or vagina is punished with imprisonment from 5 to 15 years.

###### Artigo 173º

Aggravation

If the sexual assaults referred to in art.171º and 172º are committed:

- a) Abusing authority resulting from a family relationship, guardianship or curatorship, hierarchical, economic or work dependence;
- b) Taking advantage of the functions or position that, in any capacity, they exercise in a prison establishment, educational or correctional establishment, hospital, hospice, asylum, clinic or other health establishment or other establishment intended for assistance or treatment;
- c) Against an unconscious or incapable person, particularly vulnerable due to illness, physical or mental disability;
- d) Having as victims minors under 17 years of age; the offender is punished with imprisonment from 4 to 12 years, in the case of article 171º, and with imprisonment from 5 to 20 years, in the case of article 172º.

###### SECÇÃO III

SEXUAL EXPLOITATION

###### Artigo 174º

Sexual exploitation of a third party

1. Whoever, with the intention of profit or making it a way of life, promotes, facilitates or in any way contributes to another person engaging in prostitution or practicing other sexual acts is punished with imprisonment from 3 to 10 years.

2. The offender is punished with imprisonment from 4 to 12 years, if any of the following circumstances occur:

- a) Exploitation of a situation of abandonment or economic need of the victim;
- b) Exercise of violence, serious threat or coercion against the victim;
- c) Displacement of the victim to a country different from that in which they were born or resided;
- d) Retention of any identification document of the victim.

###### Artigo 175º

Child prostitution

1. Whoever, even with the victim's consent, commits acts of sexual exploitation referred to in the previous article in relation to a minor under 17 years of age, is punished with imprisonment from 4 to 12 years in the case of number 1 and with imprisonment from 5 to 15 years in cases where any of the circumstances set out in number 2 of the previous article occur.

2. Whoever offers, obtains, seeks or delivers a minor under 17 years of age for purposes of child prostitution is punished as appropriate under another legal provision.

###### Artigo 176º

Child pornography

1. Whoever uses, for predominantly sexual purposes, exposes or represents a minor under 17 years of age in the performance of any sexual activity, real or simulated, or by any other means exhibits that sexual activity or the sexual organs of the minor, is punished with imprisonment from 3 to 10 years.

2. The same penalty applies to whoever produces, distributes, disseminates, imports, exports, offers, sells or possesses any means of communication, instrument, document or record for the purposes referred to in the previous number or with the purpose of disseminating such acts.

###### SECÇÃO IV

SEXUAL ABUSE

###### Artigo 177º

Sexual abuse of a minor

1. Whoever practices vaginal intercourse, anal intercourse or oral intercourse with a minor under 14 years of age is punished with imprisonment from 5 to 20 years.

2. Whoever practices any significant sexual act with a minor under 14 years of age is punished with imprisonment from 5 to 15 years.

###### Artigo 178º

Sexual acts with adolescents

Whoever, being an adult and outside the situations provided for in this section, practices any significant sexual act with a minor between 14 and 16 years of age, abusing their inexperience, is punished with imprisonment up to 5 years.

###### Artigo 179º

Sexual abuse of a person incapable of resistance Whoever practices a significant sexual act with an unconscious or incapable person, particularly vulnerable due to illness, physical or mental disability, taking advantage of their state of incapacity is punished with imprisonment from 4 to 12 years.

###### Artigo 180º

Sexual fraud

1. Whoever, fraudulently taking advantage of an error about their personal identity, has with another person vaginal intercourse, anal intercourse or oral intercourse or significant sexual act is punished with imprisonment up to 3 years.

2. Criminal proceedings depend on complaint.

###### Artigo 181º

Sexual exhibitionism

1. Whoever publicly harasses another person with the practice of acts of a sexual nature is punished with imprisonment up to 3 years or a fine.

2. The same penalty is incurred by those who, in front of another, practice vaginal intercourse, anal intercourse or oral intercourse, against their will and even if in private.

3. Attempt is punishable.

4. Criminal proceedings depend on complaint.

###### SECÇÃO V

COMMON PROVISIONS

###### Artigo 182º

Aggravation

1. The penalties provided for in section II to section IV of this chapter are aggravated by one third in their minimum and maximum limits, if:

- a) The victim is less than 12 years of age at the time of the commission of the acts;
- b) The offender has transmitted to the victim venereal disease, syphilis or acquired immunodeficiency syndrome;
- c) As a consequence of the acts the victim attempts or commits suicide or death results;
- d) The victim is a descendant, collateral, family member or relative by affinity up to the second degree, adopted child of the offender or person who lives with them in analogous conditions or there exists a hierarchical, economic or work dependence.
  - 2. In the case where several of the circumstances set out in the previous number occur, only one is relevant as modifying the abstract framework of the legal type, with the remaining ones being valued in the determination of the concrete penalty.

##### CAPÍTULO V

AGAINST PRIVATE LIFE

###### Artigo 183º

Disclosure

1. Whoever by any means even lawful, becomes aware of facts relating to the intimacy of the private or sexual life of another person and, without consent, publicly discloses them without just cause, is punished with imprisonment up to 1 year or a fine.

2. Criminal proceedings depend on complaint.

###### Artigo 184º

Breach of secrecy

1. Whoever, without consent, reveals another's secret of which they have become aware by reason of their status, office, employment, profession or craft, is punished with imprisonment up to 1 year or a fine.

2. If the secret is related to commercial, industrial, professional or artistic activity, of which the offender has become aware in the circumstances described above, and causes damage to another person or to the State, the penalty is 2 years of imprisonment or a fine.

3. Criminal proceedings depend on complaint.

###### Artigo 185º

Violation of domicile

1. Whoever, without consent, enters the dwelling of another person or, authorized to enter, remains there after being ordered to withdraw, is punished with imprisonment up to 2 years or a fine.

2. If the offender, to more easily commit the crime, takes advantage of the night, of the fact that the dwelling is located in a deserted place, of being 3 or more persons committing the act, uses a weapon, uses violence or threat of violence or acts by means of climbing, breaking in or false key, is punished with imprisonment up to 3 years or a fine.

3. If there are persons inside the dwelling when the offender commits the crime, the penalty provided for in the previous number aggravated by one third in its limits is applicable.

4. Attempt is punishable.

5. Criminal proceedings depend on complaint.

###### Artigo 186º

Entry into other places closed to the public

1. Whoever commits the acts described in numbers 1 and 2 of the previous article in relation to any other closed or restricted place and not freely accessible to the public, is punished, respectively, with the penalties referred to in those numbers reduced by half in the maximum limits.

2. Criminal proceedings depend on complaint.

###### Artigo 187º

Violação de correspondência ou de telecomunicações

1. Whoever, without consent or outside of procedurally admissible cases, opens a package, letter or any other writing intended for another person, or takes knowledge of its content, or prevents it from being received by its addressee, is punished with imprisonment up to 2 years or a fine.

2. The same penalty is incurred by whoever, in the same circumstances, interferes with or takes knowledge of the content of telephone, telegraphic or any other means of telecommunication communication.

3. Whoever discloses the content of letters, packages, sealed writings, telephone calls or other communications referred to in the preceding numbers, is punished with imprisonment up to 1 year or a fine, even if they had knowledge of that content in a lawful manner.

4. If the acts referred to in the preceding numbers are committed by an employee of postal, telegraph, telephone or telecommunications services, the penalties are increased by one third in their limits.

5. Criminal proceedings depend on a complaint.

#### TÍTULO III

DOS CRIMES CONTRA A VIDA EM

DEMOCRACIA

##### CAPÍTULO I

CONTRA A TRANQUILIDADE PÚBLICA

###### Artigo 188º

Associação criminosa

1. Whoever promotes or founds a group, organization or association whose purpose or activity is directed to the commission of crimes, is punished with imprisonment from 2 to 8 years.

2. A criminal group, organization or association is considered to be any grouping of two or more persons who, for a period of time and acting in concert, aim to commit or incite the commission of crimes, with the intention of disturbing public order or obtaining directly or indirectly a benefit or advantage.

3. Whoever joins, supports or participates in any of the activities of criminal groups, organizations or associations, is punished with imprisonment from 2 to 6 years.

4. Whoever leads or directs the groups, organizations or associations referred to in the preceding numbers is punished with imprisonment from 4 to 12 years.

5. The referred penalties may be extraordinarily mitigated if the agent prevents or seriously endeavors to prevent the continuation of the groups, organizations or associations, or communicates their existence to the authority so that it can prevent the commission of crimes.

###### Artigo 189º

Instigação à prática de crime

1. Whoever, publicly and by any means, incites the commission of a crime is punished with imprisonment up to 3 years or a fine.

2. Whoever, privately or publicly, praises or rewards whoever has committed any crime in such a way as, through such conduct, to incite the commission of identical crimes is punished with imprisonment up to 2 years or a fine.

3. If in the case of the preceding numbers the crime whose commission the agent had instigated is committed, the penalty of imprisonment is from 2 to 5 years, if another more serious one does not correspond to it by force of another legal provision.

###### Artigo 190º

Participação em motim

1. Whoever takes part in a public riot, during which violence against persons or property is collectively committed, is punished with imprisonment of 1 year or a fine, if another more serious penalty does not apply to them for participation in the crime committed.

2. If the agent provoked or directed the riot, they are punished with imprisonment up to 3 years.

3. If the riot is armed, the penalties referred to in the preceding numbers are doubled in their limits.

###### Artigo 191º

Impedimento do exercício de direitos políticos Whoever prevents another, through violence or threat, from exercising their political rights, is punished with imprisonment up to 1 year or a fine.

###### Artigo 192º

Tráfico de influências

1. Whoever, by themselves or through an intermediary, with their consent or ratification, solicits or accepts, for themselves or for a third party, patrimonial or non-patrimonial advantage, or its promise, to abuse their influence, real or supposed, with any public entity, is punished:

- a) With imprisonment from 2 to 6 years, if a more serious penalty does not apply by force of another legal provision, if the purpose is to obtain any favorable unlawful decision;
- b) With imprisonment up to 1 year or with a fine, if a more serious penalty does not apply by force of another legal provision, if the purpose is to obtain any favorable lawful decision.
  - 2. Whoever, by themselves or through an intermediary, with their consent or ratification, gives or promises patrimonial or non-patrimonial advantage to the persons referred to in the preceding number for the purposes provided in paragraph a) of the preceding number is punished with imprisonment up to 4 years or with a fine.

###### Artigo 193º

Desobediência a ordem de dispersão

1. Whoever does not obey a legitimate order to withdraw from a gathering or public meeting, given by a competent authority, with warning that disobedience constitutes a crime, is punished with imprisonment up to 2 years or a fine.

2. If the disobedient person is the promoter of the meeting or gathering, they are punished with imprisonment up to 3 years or a fine.

###### Artigo 194º

Abuso de sinais ou uniforme públicos

1. Whoever abusively uses a signal or call of alarm or distress, or falsely makes it believed that assistance from others is necessary due to disaster, danger or situation of collective necessity, is punished with imprisonment up to 1 year or with a fine.

2. The same penalty is incurred by whoever improperly or abusively uses uniforms, garb, attire or insignia identifying activities, authorities or public or international institutions as a means to more easily commit any offense.

###### Artigo 195º

Usurpação de funções

1. Whoever, without being authorized to do so, exercises functions or practices acts proper to an official, military command or public security force, arrogating to themselves, expressly or tacitly, such quality, shall be punished with imprisonment up to 3 years or a fine.

2. The same penalty is incurred by whoever exercises a profession for which the law requires a title or fulfillment of certain conditions, arrogating to themselves, expressly or tacitly, to possess it or fulfill them, when, in fact, they do not possess it or do not fulfill them.

3. The same penalty is incurred by whoever continues in the exercise of public functions, after having been officially notified of the dismissal or suspension of such functions.

##### CAPÍTULO II

CONTRA A SEGURANÇA DO ESTADO

###### Artigo 196º

Traição à Pátria

Whoever, by means of violence, threat of violence, usurpation or abuse of sovereign functions, prevents or attempts to prevent the exercise of national sovereignty in the territory or in part of the territory of Timor-Leste or endangers the integrity of the national territory, as a form of submission or surrender to foreign sovereignty, is punished with imprisonment from 15 to 30 years.

###### Artigo 197º

Serviço ou colaboração com forças armadas inimigas

1. The Timorese citizen who collaborates with foreign countries or groups or with their representatives, or who serves under the flag of a foreign country during war or armed action against Timor-Leste, is punished with imprisonment from 12 to 25 years.

2. Preparatory acts relating to the facts described in the preceding number are punished with imprisonment from 5 to 15 years

3. Whoever, being Timorese or resident in the national territory, commits acts suitable to help or facilitate any armed action or war against Timor-Leste by a foreign country or group, is punished with imprisonment from 10 to 20 years.

###### Artigo 198º

Sabotagem contra a defesa nacional

Whoever, with intent to harm or endanger national defense, destroys, damages or renders unusable, totally or partially:

- a) Works or materials proper to or assigned to the armed forces;
- b) Ways or means of communication or transport;
- c) Any other installations related to communications or transport; or
- d) Factories or warehouses; is punished with imprisonment from 5 to 15 years.

###### Artigo 199º

Campaign against peace effort

Whoever, being Timorese or resident in national territory, in time of preparation or war, disseminates by any means, so as to make public, rumours or statements, their own or others', which they know to be, totally or partially, false, to harm the peace effort of Timor-Leste or to assist the foreign enemy, is punished with imprisonment from 5 to 15 years.

###### Artigo 200º

Violation of State secret

1. Whoever, endangering the interest of the Timorese State relating to its external security or the conduct of its foreign policy, transmits, makes accessible to an unauthorized person or makes public a fact, document, plan, object, knowledge or any other information that should, because of that interest, remain secret in relation to a foreign country, is punished with imprisonment from 3 to 10 years.

2. Whoever collaborates with a foreign government or group with the intention of committing the acts referred to in the previous number or recruits or assists another person charged with committing them, is punished with the penalty provided for in the previous number.

3. If the agent who commits the acts described in the previous numbers exercises any political, public or military function which, by its nature, should inhibit them from committing such acts more strongly than the common citizen, is punished with imprisonment from 5 to 15 years.

###### Artigo 201º

Diplomatic infidelity

Whoever, officially representing the Timorese State, with the intention of harming national rights or interests:

- a) Conducts State business with a foreign government or international organization; or
- b) Assumes commitments in the name of Timor-Leste without being duly authorized to do so; is punished with imprisonment from 5 to 15 years.

###### Artigo 202º

Alteration of the Rule of Law

1. Whoever, by means of violence, threat of violence or incitement to civil war, attempts to destroy, alter or subjugate the constitutionally established rule of law, is punished with imprisonment from 5 to 15 years.

2. If the act described above is committed by means of armed violence, the penalty is from 5 to 20 years of imprisonment.

3. Public incitement or the distribution of weapons for the commission of the acts described above is, respectively, punished with the penalty corresponding to attempt.

###### Artigo 203º

Attack against the highest representative of a sovereign body

1. Whoever attempts against the life, physical integrity or freedom of the highest representative of a sovereign body or of whoever constitutionally replaces them or of whoever has been elected or appointed to the position, even before taking office, is punished with imprisonment from 8 to 20 years.

2. In case of consummation of a crime against life, physical integrity or freedom, the agent is punished with imprisonment from 12 to 25 years.

3. The penalties provided for in the previous numbers are correspondingly applicable whenever the acts described therein are committed against a foreign person who is in the situation referred to in number 1, ambassadors and holders of leading bodies of international organizations, when they are in Timor-Leste.

###### Artigo 204º

Coercion against constitutional bodies

1. Whoever, by violence or threat of violence, prevents or constrains the free exercise of the functions of a sovereign body is punished with imprisonment from 3 to 10 years.

2. If the acts described in the previous number are committed against district or local power bodies, the penalty is from 2 to 6 years of imprisonment.

3. If the acts referred to described in number 1 are committed against members of those bodies, the penalties referred to in numbers 1 and 2 are reduced to half of their limits.

###### Artigo 205º

Disturbance of functioning of a constitutional body

Whoever, by means of riots, disorder or shouting, unlawfully disturbs the functioning of the bodies referred to in the previous article or the exercise of functions by any of the persons who are part of them, is punished with imprisonment up to 1 year or fine.

###### Artigo 206º

Outrage of national symbols

Whoever, publicly, by words, by gestures or dissemination of writings, or by other means of communication with the public, outrages the flag or national anthem, the arms or emblems of Timorese sovereignty or fails to show the respect that is due to them, is punished with imprisonment up to 3 years or fine.

##### CAPÍTULO III

AGAINST LIFE IN SOCIETY

###### SECÇÃO I

CRIMES OF COMMON DANGER

###### Artigo 207º

Driving without a licence

Whoever uses a motorized vehicle without being qualified with the legally required licence, is punished with imprisonment up to 2 years or fine.

###### Artigo 208º

Driving under the influence of alcohol or psychotropic substances

1. Whoever, at least through negligence, drives a motorized vehicle with more than 1.2mg of alcohol per litre of blood, is punished with imprisonment up to 2 years or fine.

2. The same penalty is incurred by whoever, at least through negligence, drives a motorized vehicle, not being in conditions to do so safely, because they are under the influence of a narcotic, psychotropic substance or product with similar effect disturbing physical, mental or psychological aptitude.

###### Artigo 209º

Dangerous driving

1. Whoever drives any motorized vehicle on a public road and, by not being in conditions to do so safely or by grossly violating the rules of road traffic, creates danger to the life or physical integrity of another is punished with imprisonment from 1 to 4 years.

2. Negligence regarding the conduct or the danger is punished with imprisonment up to 2 years or fine.

###### Artigo 210º

Attack against the safety of transport

1. Whoever commits any act adequate to cause the lack or reduction of safety in means of transport and, in this way, creates danger to the life or physical integrity of another person, is punished with imprisonment from 2 to 8 years.

2. The provisions of number 2 of the previous article are correspondingly applicable.

###### Artigo 211º

Prohibited weapons

1. Whoever, outside legal prescriptions, manufactures, imports, transports, sells or transfers to another firearms, chemical weapons, biological weapons, nuclear weapons, ammunition for those, substances for their manufacture or operation or any other type of explosive, is punished with imprisonment from 2 to 6 years.

2. If the acts described in the previous number have as their purpose the use for war purposes, the penalty is from 2 to 8 years of imprisonment.

3. The simple possession, use or carrying of a firearm without the agent being legally authorized, is punishable with imprisonment up to 2 years or fine.

###### Artigo 212º

Drunkenness and intoxication

1. Whoever, at least through negligence, places themselves in a state of non-imputability derived from the ingestion or consumption of alcoholic beverage or toxic substance and in that state commits a typical unlawful act is punished with imprisonment up to 5 years or fine.

2. The penalty cannot be greater than that provided for the typical unlawful act committed.

3. Criminal proceedings depend on complaint.

###### Artigo 213º

Qualification for the exercise of certain activities

1. Whoever, without being legally qualified, sells, administers, prescribes or transfers in any way, habitually, to other persons, pharmaceutical or other products whose commerce and prescription, administration or transfer, are reserved to health professionals or other duly licensed entities, is punished with imprisonment up to 3 years or fine.

2. If as a consequence of the previous acts danger to the life of another person results, the penalty is from 1 to 4 years of imprisonment.

###### Artigo 214º

Adulterated or deteriorated products

1. Whoever puts up for sale, administers or transfers in any way to another person food products, pharmaceuticals, or others which, being deteriorated, adulterated or contaminated, are likely to endanger life shall be punished with imprisonment from 2 to 8 years.

2. If the commission of the acts described in the preceding paragraph results in the death of the victim due to the consumption of such products, the penalty shall be from 3 to 12 years.

###### SECÇÃO II

CRIMES AGAINST THE ENVIRONMENT

###### Artigo 215º

Against the environment

1. Whoever, not complying with legal or regulatory provisions protecting the environment, causes or carries out directly or indirectly emissions, discharges, radiations, extractions or excavations, fires, noises, vibrations, injections or deposits, in the atmosphere, in the soil, in the subsoil or in terrestrial, maritime or underground waters, including in cross-border zones, or water abstraction that may seriously harm the balance of natural systems, shall be punished with imprisonment up to 3 years or a fine.

2. If the agent intentionally releases, emits or introduces ionizing radiations or other substances into the air, land or maritime, continental, surface or underground waters, in a quantity that produces in one or more persons serious bodily harm requiring medical or surgical treatment or produces irreversible sequelae, shall be punished with imprisonment from 2 to 8 years and if death is caused the penalty shall be from 5 to 15 years' imprisonment.

###### Artigo 216º

Aggravation

1. If the acts or activities provided for in the preceding article are carried out by an industrial or commercial establishment and any of the following circumstances occur:

- a) The industry or commercial activity operates clandestinely without the proper administrative license or authorization
- b) When express orders from the competent administrative authority for the correction or suspension of the activities referred to in the preceding article have been disobeyed
- c) When the safety rules or procedures provided for in legal or regulatory provisions have not been complied with.
- d) When, by any means, the inspection process of the competent administrative authority has been intentionally prevented or information about the environmental consequences of the industry or commercial activity has been omitted or falsified.
- e) When an irreversible or catastrophic state of environmental deterioration has been produced, the penalties provided for in the preceding paragraph are aggravated by one third in their minimum and maximum limits.
  - 2. Criminally liable, under the terms of the preceding paragraph, are the individual holder, the legal representatives, or those who act on behalf of the legal person holding the offending industrial or commercial establishments, as well as the partners or members who authorize them to act, when the latter is irregularly constituted.

###### Artigo 217º

Against flora or fauna

1. Whoever with serious harm to the environment cuts, burns, uproots, collects or carries out illegal trafficking of any species of flora or its seeds, classified as threatened or at risk of extinction, destroying or seriously altering its natural habitat, shall be punished with imprisonment up to 3 years or a fine.

2. The penalty provided for in the preceding paragraph shall be incurred by whoever introduces or facilitates the entry of non-native species of flora or fauna, in such a way as to harm the biological balance, violating the laws or provisions of a general nature protecting species of flora or fauna.

###### Artigo 218º

Against threatened or endangered species

1. Whoever hunts or fishes threatened species or carries out any activity that prevents their development or hinders their reproduction or migration, violating laws or provisions of a general nature protecting wild fauna species, as well as commercializes or traffics them, in whole or in part, shall be punished with imprisonment up to 3 years or a fine.

2. If the acts referred to in the preceding paragraph are carried out:

- a) In terrestrial or maritime zones declared protected natural zones;
- b) Against species or subspecies classified as endangered; the agent shall be punished with imprisonment up to 5 years or a fine.

###### Artigo 219º

Illegal fishing

1. Whoever fishes in national maritime waters without the proper fishing license obtained from the competent administrative entity shall be punished with imprisonment up to 3 years or a fine.

2. If the agent is a legal person, criminally liable, under the terms of the preceding paragraph, are the legal representatives, or those who act on behalf of the legal person, as well as the partners or members who authorize them to act, when the latter is irregularly constituted.

3. Fishing carried out for domestic subsistence is not punishable under paragraph one.

###### Artigo 220º

Illicit fishing methods

Whoever uses firearms, explosives, toxic substances or other instruments or similar arts of destructive efficacy for maritime fauna, for the capture of fishery resources, in national terrestrial or maritime waters, shall be punished with imprisonment up to 5 years or a fine.

###### Artigo 221º

Prohibited burning
