1. Whoever carries out burning outside the appropriate season or without administrative authorization, when necessary, resulting in the destruction of forest, plantation or crop shall be punished with imprisonment up to 2 years or a fine.

2. If the burning is legally carried out but, through negligence, the agent causes the damage referred to in the preceding paragraph, the penalty shall be imprisonment up to 1 year or a fine.

###### SECÇÃO III

OTHER CRIMES

###### Artigo 222º

Impediment or disturbance of procession, ceremony or worship

1. Whoever prevents or disturbs the holding of a procession or funeral ceremony or the exercise of religious worship by means of violence or threat of violence or any other form of coercion shall be punished with imprisonment up to 2 years or a fine.

2. Criminal proceedings depend on a complaint.

###### Artigo 223º

Desecration of object or place of worship or veneration

1. Whoever, in a manner to provoke alarm or disturb social peace, desecrates a place or object of worship or religious veneration shall be punished with imprisonment up to 2 years or a fine.

2. The same penalty shall be incurred by whoever offends or insults another person because of their belief or religious function, in a manner suitable to provoke alarm or social disturbance.

3. Criminal proceedings depend on a complaint.

###### Artigo 224º

Destruction, removal, concealment or desecration of corpse

1. Whoever, against or without the consent of the rightful person and outside the cases in which the law permits it, removes, destroys or conceals corpses or parts thereof, or ashes of a deceased person, shall be punished with imprisonment up to 2 years or a fine.

2. The same penalty shall be incurred by whoever desecrates corpses, parts of corpses or ashes of deceased persons, committing acts offensive to the respect due to the dead.

3. Attempt is punishable.

4. Criminal proceedings depend on a complaint.

###### Artigo 225º

Non-compliance with maintenance obligation

1. Whoever is obliged to provide maintenance, has the means to do so and fails to comply with the obligation in such a way as to endanger the satisfaction of the fundamental needs of the person entitled to maintenance shall be punished with imprisonment up to 3 years or a fine, even if assistance provided by another person averts the danger.

2. Criminal proceedings depend on a complaint.

###### Artigo 226º

Abduction of minor

1. Whoever abducts or refuses to hand over a minor to the person to whom their custody has been entrusted or induces the minor to flee shall be punished with imprisonment up to 3 years or a fine.

2. If the acts are committed with violence or threat of violence, the penalty shall be imprisonment from 1 to 4 years.

3. Criminal proceedings depend on a complaint.

###### Article 227

Failure to provide assistance

1. Whoever, in a case of serious need, namely caused by disaster, accident, public calamity or situation of common danger, which endangers the life, physical integrity or freedom of another person, fails to provide the necessary assistance to remove the danger, whether by personal action or by promoting rescue, shall be punished with imprisonment up to 1 year or a fine.

2. If the situation referred to in the previous number was created by the person who omits the due assistance, the omitter shall be punished with imprisonment up to 2 years or a fine.

3. The failure to provide assistance is not punishable when there is serious risk to the life or physical integrity of the omitter or when, for another relevant reason, assistance cannot be required of them.

4. Criminal proceedings depend on a complaint.

###### Article 228

Refusal of medical assistance

A doctor or health professional who refuses the assistance of their profession in case of danger to life or serious danger to the physical integrity of another person, which cannot be removed in another way, shall be punished with imprisonment up to 3 years or a fine.

##### CHAPTER IV

ELECTORAL CRIMES

###### Article 229

Fraud in registration

1. Whoever prevents another person, who they know has the right, from registering, records facts that they know are not true, omits facts that they should record or by any other means falsifies the electoral registration, shall be punished with imprisonment up to 3 years or a fine.

2. If the person is prevented from registering or convinced to register by means of violence or cunningly provoked deception, the applicable penalty is imprisonment from 2 to 6 years.

3. The attempt is punishable.

###### Article 230

Obstruction to candidacy

Whoever, by any means, prevents another person, party or political force that they know has the right to run for electoral act, shall be punished with imprisonment from 2 to 8 years.

###### Article 231

Ineligible candidate

1. Whoever, knowing that they do not have electoral capacity to be elected, submits their candidacy, shall be punished with imprisonment up to 1 year or a fine.

2. The attempt is punishable.

###### Article 232

Lack of electoral registers

Whoever, being in charge of preparing or correcting electoral registers, does not proceed with their execution or prevents the legal substitute from doing so in order to prevent the holding of an electoral act, shall be punished with imprisonment up to 3 years or a fine.

###### Article 233

Unlawful electoral propaganda

1. Whoever uses a legally prohibited means of propaganda or continues to carry out propaganda beyond the established deadline or in a prohibited place, shall be punished with imprisonment up to 1 year or a fine.

2. Whoever prevents the exercise of the right to electoral propaganda or proceeds with its unlawful destruction, shall be punished with imprisonment up to 2 years or a fine.

###### Article 234

Obstruction to freedom of choice

1. Whoever, by means of violence, threat of violence or by fraudulent artifice, compels another person not to vote or to vote in a certain way or buys or sells votes, shall be punished with imprisonment up to 3 years or a fine.

2. The same penalty applies to whoever, requested to assist in voting a blind person or someone who is legally entitled to such, disrespects the voting direction communicated to them.

3. The attempt is punishable.

###### Article 235

Disturbance of electoral act

1. Whoever, by any means, disturbs the functioning of the polling station, shall be punished with imprisonment up to 1 year or a fine.

2. The perpetrator shall be punished with imprisonment from 2 to 6 years if the disturbance results from:

- a) Violence or threat of violence;
- b) Tumult or population gathering near the polling station;
- c) Intentional cutting of electric power;
- d) Absence of someone indispensable to the act if, for this reason, the holding of the act must be considered seriously affected to begin or continue.
  - 3. The provisions of the previous numbers apply correspondingly if the facts are committed during the counting of results after the voting has taken place.

###### Article 236

Obstruction to supervision of electoral act

1. Whoever, by any means, prevents the representative of any party or political force, legally constituted and running in the electoral act, from exercising their supervisory powers, shall be punished with imprisonment up to 3 years or a fine.

2. The attempt is punishable.

###### Article 237

Fraud in voting

1. Whoever votes without having the right to vote or does so more than once in relation to the same electoral act shall be punished with imprisonment up to 3 years or a fine.

2. The same penalty is incurred by whoever intentionally permits the commission of the act described in the previous number.

3. The attempt is punishable.

###### Article 238

Fraud in scrutiny

1. Whoever, by any means, vitiates the counting of votes in the act of counting or publication of electoral results, shall be punished with imprisonment from 2 to 6 years.

2. The same penalty is incurred by whoever, with fraudulent intent, substitutes, destroys, suppresses, violates, vitiates or falsifies voting or counting ballots, or documents relating to the election.

###### Article 239

Refusal of electoral office

Whoever is appointed to be part of polling station tables and, without justification, refuses to assume or abandons those functions, shall be punished with imprisonment up to 1 year or a fine.

###### Article 240

Violation of secrecy of ballot

Whoever, in an electoral act carried out by secret ballot, violates such secrecy, taking or giving knowledge of the voting direction of another person, shall be punished with imprisonment up to 1 year or a fine.

###### Article 241

Duty of neutrality and impartiality

Electoral administration officials or those who collaborate with it who violate the duties of neutrality and impartiality shall be punished with imprisonment up to 2 years or a fine.

###### Article 242

Aggravation

If whoever commits any of the crimes provided for in this chapter performs public functions, namely in the Government, in the National Parliament, in the Armed Forces, as a judicial magistrate or of the Public Prosecutor's Office, in the various police forces or in other administrative bodies, the sanctions provided for in the type are raised to double their limits.

##### CHAPTER V

AGAINST PUBLIC AUTHORITY

###### Article 243

Obstruction to public authority

1. Whoever, by means of violence or serious threat against an official or agent of military, militarized or police forces, opposes the practice of an act relating to the exercise of their functions or compels the practice of an act contrary to their duties shall be punished with imprisonment from 2 to 6 years.

2. If the act referred to in the previous number is actually performed or prevented from being performed, the penalty is from 2 to 8 years.

###### Article 244

Disobedience

1. Whoever fails or persists in failing to obey a legitimate order or mandate, regularly communicated and coming from a competent authority or official, shall be punished with imprisonment up to 3 years or a fine, if:

- a) A legal provision so determines; or
- b) If warned that their conduct is liable to generate criminal liability and the authority or official makes the corresponding commination.
  - 2. In cases where the legal provision qualifies the act as qualified disobedience, the penalty is from 1 to 4 years of imprisonment.
  - 3. Disobedience to specific prohibitions or interdictions determined in a criminal sentence, as well as disobedience to ancillary penalties or non-custodial security measures, is punishable with the penalty referred to in number 1.

###### Article 245

Release of prisoners

1. Whoever, by illegal means, releases or, by any means, assists the escape of a person legally deprived of liberty, shall be punished with imprisonment from 2 to 6 years.

2. If the acts are committed with the use of violence, using weapons or with the collaboration of more than two persons, the penalty is imprisonment from 2 to 8 years.

###### Artigo 246º

Escape

1. Whoever, being legally deprived of liberty, escapes, is punished with imprisonment from 1 to 4 years.

2. If the escape is achieved by any of the means described in number 2 of the previous article, the penalty is from 2 to 6 years.

###### Artigo 247º

Official's assistance in escape

1. The official who assists in the commission of any of the acts provided for in the previous articles is punished with the penalties therein prescribed aggravated by one third in their limits.

2. If the official was supposed to exercise custody or surveillance over the escapee and, even so, assisted in those acts, the prescribed penalties are aggravated by half in their limits.

3. If the escape is due only to gross negligence of the official in charge of surveillance of the escapee, the penalty is imprisonment up to 3 years or a fine.

###### Artigo 248º

Riot of prisoners

1. Those who, being legally deprived of liberty, in concert and in joint effort with another person in the same circumstances, attack or threaten with violence those in charge of their surveillance or custody, to achieve their escape or that of a third party, or to commit an act or refrain from its commission, are punished with imprisonment from 2 to 8 years.

2. If through the commission of the acts described in the previous number the intents of one's own or another's escape are achieved, the penalty is from 3 to 10 years.

###### Artigo 249º

Misappropriation or destruction of objects under public authority

Whoever destroys, damages or renders useless, totally or partially, or in any way, removes from public authority, to which it is assigned or subject, a document or other movable object, as well as a thing that has been attached, seized or subject to a precautionary measure, is punished with imprisonment from 2 to 6 years, if a more severe penalty does not apply by force of another provision.

###### Artigo 250º

Breaking of marks, seals and notices

1. Whoever opens, breaks or renders useless, totally or partially, marks or seals, legitimately affixed by a competent official, to identify or maintain inviolable any thing, or to certify that it has been subject to attachment, seizure or precautionary measure, is punished with imprisonment up to 3 years or a fine.

2. Whoever in any way intentionally prevents the addressee from becoming aware of a notice posted by a competent official is punished with imprisonment up to 2 years or a fine.

#### TÍTULO IV

OF CRIMES AGAINST PROPERTY

##### CAPÍTULO I

AGAINST PROPERTY

###### Artigo 251º

Simple theft

1. Whoever, with unlawful intent of appropriation for oneself or for another, removes another's movable property, is punished with imprisonment up to 3 years or a fine.

2. The attempt is punishable.

3. Criminal proceedings depend on complaint.

###### Artigo 252º

Aggravated theft

1. The agent who commits the acts referred to in the previous article is punished with imprisonment from 2 to 8 years, if:

- a) The movable property of another has high scientific, artistic or historical value, or is important for economic or technological development;
- b) The movable property of another is a vehicle, transported in a vehicle or by a passenger of public transport, or is located at a dock or terminal for boarding and disembarking;
- c) The movable property of another is assigned to religious worship or to the veneration of the memory of the dead and is located in a place intended for worship or in a cemetery;
- d) The agent takes advantage of the night to more easily commit the theft;
- e) The victim suffers considerable losses as a consequence of the appropriation;
- f) The agent enters a dwelling, public service facilities, commercial or industrial establishment with intent to steal;
- g) The agent uses false keys, climbing or breaking in the execution of their design;
- h) The agent takes advantage of the situation of special vulnerability of the victim of disaster, accident or public calamity;
- i) The agent takes advantage of the existence of a special relationship of trust with the victim or with the owner of the place where the removed property is located;
- j) The agent makes the commission of thefts a way of life;
- k) The crime is committed by 3 or more persons, including the agent;
- l) The value of the removed property exceeds 1,000 US dollars but is less than or equal to 5,000 US dollars.
  - 2. If the value of the removed property exceeds 5,000 US dollars, the imprisonment penalty shall be from 3 to 10 years.
  - 3. If in the same conduct more than one of the circumstances referred to in number one concur, only the one having the strongest aggravating effect is considered for the purpose of determining the abstract framework of the applicable legal type, with the remaining ones being valued as circumstances of a general nature in determining the measure of the penalty.
  - 4. If the value of the stolen property is less than 50 US dollars, the circumstances referred to in no. 1 only function as aggravating circumstances of a general nature.

###### Artigo 253º

Robbery

1. Whoever, with unlawful intent of appropriation for oneself or for another person, removes or compels the delivery of another's movable property by means of violence against a person, threat with imminent danger to life or physical integrity, or placing them in the impossibility of resisting, is punished with imprisonment from 3 to 10 years.

2. If, simultaneously with the agent's conduct, any of the circumstances described in number 1 of the previous article occurs, the imprisonment penalty shall be from 4 to 12 years.

3. If from the agent's conduct danger to the victim's life results or serious offenses to physical integrity are caused, the agent is punished with imprisonment from 5 to 15 years.

4. If from the agent's conduct death results, the agent is punished with imprisonment from 5 to 20 years.

###### Artigo 254º

Violence after removal

Whoever, caught in flagrante delicto of theft, acts in the forms described in the previous article with the purpose of retaining or preventing the restitution of the appropriated things, is punished with the penalties for the crime of robbery respectively.

###### Artigo 255º

Theft of use of vehicle

1. Whoever uses an automobile or other motorized vehicle, aircraft, boat or bicycle without authorization from the rightful person, is punished with imprisonment up to 2 years or a fine.

2. The attempt is punishable.

3. Criminal proceedings depend on complaint.

###### Artigo 256º

Simple breach of trust

1. Whoever unlawfully appropriates movable property that has been delivered to them under a title not transferring ownership is punished with imprisonment up to 3 years or a fine.

2. The attempt is punishable.

3. Criminal proceedings depend on complaint.

###### Artigo 257º

Aggravated breach of trust

1. If the movable property has a value exceeding 1,000 US dollars, the agent is punished with imprisonment from 2 to 8 years.

2. The penalties provided for in the previous number and in article 256 are increased by one third in their minimum and maximum limits if the agent received the property in deposit imposed by law, by reason of office, employment or profession, or in the capacity of guardian, curator or depositary.

###### Artigo 258º

Simple damage

1. Whoever, totally or partially, destroys, damages, disfigures or renders useless another's property is punished with imprisonment up to 3 years or a fine.

2. The attempt is punishable.

3. Criminal proceedings depend on complaint.

###### Artigo 259º

Aggravated damage

Whoever, totally or partially, destroys, damages, disfigures or renders useless a thing:

- a) Intended for public use or utility;
- b) With an important scientific, artistic or historical value or of great importance for technological or scientific development;
- c) Corresponding to a means of communication or transportation of great social importance;
- d) Causing a loss exceeding 1,000 US dollars;
- e) Another's, assigned to religious worship or to the veneration of the memory of the dead and located in a place intended for worship or in a cemetery; is punished with imprisonment from 2 to 8 years or a fine.

###### Artigo 260º

Damage with violence

If the acts described in articles 258º and 259º are committed with violence against a person, or threat with imminent danger to life or physical integrity, or rendering them unable to resist, their conduct is punished with a prison sentence of 4 to 12 years.

###### Artigo 261º

Usurpation of immovable property

1. Whoever, by means of violence or serious threat against another person, invades or occupies another's immovable property, with the intention of exercising a right of ownership, possession, use or easement not protected by law, judgment, contract or administrative act, is punished with a prison sentence of 1 to 4 years.

2. If the means employed constitutes a crime punishable with a penalty greater than that set out in the previous number, that shall be the applicable penalty.

###### Artigo 262º

Alteration of boundary markers

1. Whoever, with the intention of appropriating, totally or partially, another's immovable property, for themselves or for another person, removes or alters a boundary marker or any other sign intended to establish limits of immovable property, is punished with a prison sentence of up to 1 year or a fine.

2. Criminal proceedings depend on a complaint.

###### Artigo 263º

Arson

1. Whoever, intending to cause fire in a house, building, establishment, means of transport, forest, plantation or any other property and, in this way, create danger to life, physical integrity or to patrimonial assets valued at more than 5,000 US dollars, is punished with a prison sentence of 2 to 8 years.

2. If the acts described in the previous number concern public property or in which public services operate, the penalty is 2 to 10 years of imprisonment.

3. The agent is punished with a prison sentence of up to 3 years or a fine if the acts are committed with negligence, unless a more severe penalty corresponds to them by force of another legal provision.

4. If only the danger referred to in no. 1 is created by negligence, the penalty is 2 to 6 years of imprisonment.

5. In the cases provided for in no. 4, criminal proceedings depend on a complaint.

###### Artigo 264º

Active repentance

If, after the commission of the crimes provided for in articles 251º, 252º, 256º to 261º and 263º, no. 4, but before the trial hearing has begun, the agent performs acts aimed at restitution or full or partial reparation of the damages caused, the penalty may be extraordinarily mitigated.

###### Artigo 265º

Definitions

For the purposes of this Code, the following are considered:

- a) "Breaking and entering" the breaking, fracture or destruction, in whole or in part, of a device intended to close or prevent entry, externally or internally, to a house or enclosed place dependent on it;
- b) "Climbing" the introduction into a house or enclosed place dependent on it, through a place not normally intended for entry or through any device intended to close or prevent entry or passage;
- c) "False keys",
- i) Those imitated, counterfeited or altered;
  - ii) The genuine ones when, fortuitously or surreptitiously, they are outside the power of those who have the right to use them; and
  - iii) Lockpicks or any instruments that may serve to open locks or other security devices.

##### CAPÍTULO II

AGAINST PROPERTY IN GENERAL

###### Artigo 266º

Simple fraud

1. Whoever, with the intention of obtaining for themselves or for a third party unlawful enrichment, by means of error or deception about facts which they cunningly provoked, causes another to perform acts that cause them, or cause another person, patrimonial damage, is punished with a prison sentence of up to 3 years or a fine.

2. The attempt is punishable.

3. The provisions of article 264º are correspondingly applicable.

4. Criminal proceedings depend on a complaint.

###### Artigo 267º

Aggravated fraud

1. Whoever, as a result of the conduct described in the previous article:

- a) Causes damage exceeding 2,000 US dollars;
- b) Makes a way of life out of the practice of fraud;
- c) Places the injured person in a difficult economic situation; is punished with a prison sentence of 3 to 10 years.
  - 2. The provisions of article 264º are correspondingly applicable.

###### Artigo 268º

Computer fraud

1. Whoever, with the intention of obtaining for themselves or for a third party unlawful enrichment, causes another person patrimonial damage, by interfering with the result of computer data processing or through incorrect structuring of a computer program, incorrect or incomplete use of data, unauthorized use of data or intervention in any other unauthorized manner in the processing, is punished with a prison sentence of up to 3 years or a fine.

2. The attempt is punishable.

3. Criminal proceedings depend on a complaint.

###### Artigo 269º

Aggravated computer fraud

When as a result of the conduct described in the previous article any of the circumstances described in no. 1 of article 267º occurs, the agent is punished with a prison sentence of 3 to 10 years.

###### Artigo 270º

Extortion

1. Whoever, with the intention of obtaining for themselves or for a third party unlawful enrichment, compels another person, by means of violence or threat of significant harm, to a patrimonial disposition that entails, for them or for another, damage, is punished with a prison sentence of 2 to 6 years.

2. If the circumstances provided for in article 252º occur, the conduct of the agent is punished with the penalties set out therein.

3. Criminal proceedings depend on a complaint.

###### Artigo 271º

Simple receiving

1. Whoever, without having previously ensured its lawful origin, acquires or receives, under any title, a thing which, by its nature or by the quality of the person who holds it or offers it, or by the amount of the price or conditions of sale or offer, causes a reasonably diligent person to suspect that it comes from criminal conduct against another person's property, is punished with a prison sentence of up to 2 years or with a fine.

2. The proceedings depend on a complaint.

###### Artigo 272º

Aggravated receiving

1. Whoever, with the intention of obtaining, for themselves or for another person, patrimonial advantage, concealing a thing obtained by another through a crime against property, receives it, pledges it, acquires it under any title, holds it, preserves it, transmits it or contributes to transmitting it, or in any other way ensures, for themselves or for a third party, its possession or the value or product directly resulting from it, is punished with a prison sentence of 2 to 8 years.

2. The agent is punished for the practice of the acts described in the previous number with a prison sentence of 3 to 10 years, if:

- a) They make receiving a way of life or practice it habitually;
- b) The goods, values or products have a value exceeding 2,000 US dollars;
- c) If, at the time of receiving, the receiver had knowledge that the crime was committed with any of the circumstances referred to in article 253º, no. 1.

###### Artigo 273º

Assistance to the criminal

1. Whoever, after the commission of a crime against property, helps the agent of the crime to take advantage of the thing thus obtained or of a benefit directly resulting from the appropriated thing, is punished with a prison sentence of up to 1 year or a fine.

2. Criminal proceedings depend on a complaint.

###### Artigo 274º

Harmful administration

1. Whoever is in charge of disposing of or administering another's interests, services or patrimonial assets, even if they are a partner of the company or legal person to which those assets, interests or services belong, and by having intentionally breached the rules of control and management or by having acted with serious violation of the duties inherent to the function, causes economically significant patrimonial damage, is punished with a prison sentence of 1 to 4 years.

2. If the assets, interests or services referred to in the previous number belong to the State, a legal person of public utility, a cooperative or popular association, the penalty of 2 to 6 years of imprisonment is applicable to the agent.

3. The same penalties are applicable to whoever appropriates or allows the unlawful appropriation of things which they could only dispose of within the scope and for the purposes inherent to those who administer another's property.

###### Artigo 275º

Negligent administration

1. Whoever, being in the conditions described in paragraph 1 of the preceding article, causes serious patrimonial damage by failing to act with the diligence to which they were bound according to the circumstances and of which they were capable, is punished with imprisonment for up to 1 year or a fine.

2. A penalty of imprisonment for up to 2 years or a fine is applied if the assets or interests concern any of the situations described in paragraph 2 of the preceding article.

3. Criminal proceedings depend on a complaint.

###### Artigo 276º

Intentional bankruptcy or insolvency

1. Whoever, by any means, leads a company to bankruptcy or places themselves in a situation of insolvency, with the intention of harming creditors, if bankruptcy or insolvency is declared, is punished with imprisonment from 2 to 8 years.

2. If the facts described in the preceding paragraph concern public enterprises or cooperatives, the penalty is aggravated by one third in its minimum and maximum limits.

###### Artigo 277º

Negligent bankruptcy or insolvency

1. Whoever causes bankruptcy or insolvency through serious carelessness or imprudence, prodigality or manifestly excessive expenses, or through gross negligence in the exercise of their activity, is punished with imprisonment for up to 2 years or a fine, if bankruptcy or insolvency is declared.

2. Criminal proceedings depend on a complaint.

#### TÍTULO V

OF CRIMES AGAINST THE ADMINISTRATION OF

JUSTICE

###### Artigo 278º

False testimony or statement

1. Whoever gives testimony as a party, making false statements in relation to facts on which they must testify, after having taken an oath and having been warned of the criminal consequences to which they are exposed by giving false testimony, is punished with imprisonment for up to 3 years or a fine.

2. The same penalty is incurred by the suspect or defendant in relation to statements about their identity and criminal record.

3. If, as a consequence of the conduct described in the preceding paragraphs, someone is deprived of liberty, the agent is punished with imprisonment from 2 to 8 years.

###### Artigo 279º

False witness testimony, expert opinion, interpretation or translation

1. Whoever, as a witness, expert, technician, translator or interpreter, before a court or official competent to receive as evidence testimony, report, information or translation, gives testimony, presents a report, provides information or makes false translations, is punished with imprisonment for up to 4 years or a fine.

2. The same penalty is incurred by whoever, without just cause, refuses to testify or to present a report, information or translation.

3. If the fact referred to in paragraph 1 is committed after the agent has taken an oath and has been warned of the criminal consequences to which they are exposed, the penalty is imprisonment for up to 5 years.

4. If, as a consequence of the conduct described in the preceding paragraphs, someone is deprived of liberty, the agent is punished with imprisonment from 2 to 8 years.

###### Artigo 280º

Equivalence to withdrawal

The repentance and retraction of the agent who has committed any of the facts described in the preceding article before the effects of the falsehood have been taken into account in the decision or have caused harm to another person, are equivalent to withdrawal.

###### Artigo 281º

Bribery

1. Whoever convinces or attempts to convince another person, through a gift or promise of patrimonial or non-patrimonial advantage, to commit any of the facts referred to in articles 278 or 279, is punished with imprisonment for up to 4 years, when such act occurs.

2. If the commission of the facts referred to in articles 278 and 279 does not materialize, the agent is punished with imprisonment for up to 3 years or a fine

###### Artigo 282º

Denial of justice

1. The official who, within the scope of procedural inquiry, judicial proceedings, for administrative offences or disciplinary proceedings, knowingly decides or fails to decide, promotes or fails to promote, investigates or fails to investigate or performs an act in the exercise of their functions, contrary to law, is punished with imprisonment for up to 3 years or a fine.

2. If the fact described in the preceding paragraph is committed with the intention of harming or benefiting someone, the official is punished with imprisonment for up to 5 years.

3. If the conduct described in the preceding paragraphs results in the deprivation of liberty of a person, the agent is punished with imprisonment from 2 to 8 years.

4. If the preceding conduct is committed with gross negligence, the penalties are reduced by half in their minimum and maximum limits.

###### Artigo 283º

Coercion of magistrate

1. Whoever, by means of violence, threat of important harm or by any other means, acts so as to prevent a judge or public prosecutor from freely exercising their functions, is punished with imprisonment from 1 to 4 years.

2. If the agent commits the facts by taking advantage of being invested with a political, public, military or police position, the penalty is 2 to 8 years of imprisonment.

3. If, as a consequence of the conduct described in the preceding paragraphs, the magistrate omits or performs an act in violation of express law resulting in harm to third parties, the penalty is 3 to 10 years of imprisonment.

###### Artigo 284º

Obstruction of judicial activity

1. Whoever, by any means, opposes, hinders or prevents the compliance with or execution of a judicial decision that has become final, is punished with imprisonment from 2 to 5 years.

2. If the agent who commits the facts described in the preceding paragraphs exercises any political, public or military function that, by its nature, should have prevented them from committing such acts, they are punished with imprisonment from 2 to 8 years.

###### Artigo 285º

False accusation

1. Whoever, by any means, before an authority or publicly, with awareness of the falsity of the imputation, reports or casts upon a specific person the suspicion of having committed a crime, with the intention that criminal proceedings be instituted against them, is punished with imprisonment for up to 3 years or a fine.

2. If the false imputation refers to an administrative offence or disciplinary matter, the penalty shall be extraordinarily mitigated.

3. If the facts described in the preceding paragraphs are deliberately promoted by an official charged with instituting the respective proceedings, the applicable penalties are aggravated by one third in their minimum and maximum limits.

###### Artigo 286º

Failure to report

Whoever, having knowledge of the commission of a public crime and, being obliged to report it, fails to do so, is punished with the penalty corresponding to the crime they concealed reduced by two thirds in its minimum and maximum limits.

###### Artigo 287º

Malfeasance of magistrate or official

1. The judge, public prosecutor or official who at any stage of judicial proceedings, with the intention of benefiting or harming another person, performs any act within the scope of the functional powers of which they are the holder or omits an act that they should perform, knowingly and contrary to law, is punished with imprisonment from 2 to 6 years.

2. If the facts described in the preceding paragraph result in the deprivation of liberty of a person or cause a situation of illegal imprisonment or detention, the penalty is 3 to 10 years of imprisonment.

###### Artigo 288º

Malfeasance of lawyer or public defender

1. The lawyer or public defender who intentionally harms a case entrusted to their representation is punished with imprisonment from 1 to 4 years.

2. The lawyer or public defender who, in the same case, advocates for or exercises the defense of persons whose interests are in conflict, with the intention of acting for the benefit or detriment of any of them, is punished with imprisonment from 2 to 6 years.

###### Artigo 289º

Simulation of crime

1. Whoever, without imputing it to a specific person, reports a crime or creates suspicion of its commission to the competent authority, knowing that it did not occur, shall be punished with a sentence of imprisonment of up to 2 years or a fine.

2. If the fact relates to a contravention, administrative offence or disciplinary wrongdoing, the offender shall be punished with a sentence of imprisonment of up to 1 year or a fine.

3. If the acts described in the preceding paragraphs are committed by officials responsible for instituting the respective proceedings, the applicable penalties shall be increased by one third of their limits.

###### Artigo 290º

Personal favouritism

1. Whoever, in whole or in part, prevents, frustrates or evades the evidentiary or preventive activity of the competent authority, with the intention or awareness of preventing another person who has committed a crime from being subjected to a penalty or security measure, shall be punished with a sentence of imprisonment of up to 3 years or a fine.

2. Attempt is punishable.

3. If the favouritism is committed by an official who participates or has competence to participate in the proceedings or who is responsible for executing a penalty or security measure or for ordering its execution, the penalty shall be 2 to 5 years of imprisonment.

4. The commission of the acts described in paragraph 1 shall not be punishable when:

- a) The offender, by the act, seeks at the same time to prevent a penalty or security measure from being applied or executed against himself;
- b) The offender is the spouse, descendant, ascendant, adopter or adoptee, relative or relative by affinity up to the second degree of the person for whose benefit the act was performed, or someone who lives with that person in a situation analogous to that of spouses.

###### Artigo 291º

Violation of judicial secrecy

1. Whoever, in violation of a legal provision and without just cause, makes public the content of a criminal procedural act covered by judicial secrecy or in respect of which a decision has been made to exclude publicity, shall be punished with a sentence of imprisonment of 1 to 4 years.

2. If the violation is committed by means of a media outlet, the penalty shall be 2 to 6 years of imprisonment.

#### TÍTULO VI

OF CRIMES COMMITTED IN THE EXERCISE OF

PUBLIC FUNCTIONS

###### Artigo 292º

Passive corruption for unlawful act

1. An official who, by himself or through an intermediary, with his consent or ratification, solicits or accepts, for himself or for a third party, without it being due to him, a patrimonial or non-patrimonial advantage, or its promise, for any act or omission contrary to the duties of office, even if prior to that solicitation or acceptance, shall be punished with a sentence of imprisonment of 3 to 15 years.

2. If the offender, before committing the act, voluntarily repudiates the offer or promise that he had accepted, or returns the advantage, or, in the case of a fungible thing, its value, he shall be exempt from penalty.

3. The penalty shall be specially mitigated if the offender concretely assists in gathering decisive evidence for the identification or capture of other perpetrators.

###### Artigo 293º

Passive corruption for lawful act

1. An official who, by himself or through an intermediary, with his consent or ratification, solicits or accepts, for himself or for a third party, without it being due to him, a patrimonial or non-patrimonial advantage, or its promise, for any act or omission not contrary to the duties of office, even if prior to that solicitation or acceptance, shall be punished with a sentence of imprisonment of up to 3 years or with a fine.

2. The same penalty shall be incurred by an official who, by himself or through an intermediary, with his consent or ratification, solicits or accepts, for himself or for a third party, without it being due to him, a patrimonial or non-patrimonial advantage from a person who has had, has or will have any claim before him dependent on the exercise of his public functions.

###### Artigo 294º

Active corruption

1. Whoever, by himself or through an intermediary with his consent or ratification, gives or promises to an official, or to a third party with the knowledge of the official, a patrimonial or non-patrimonial advantage not due to the official, for the purpose indicated in article 292º, shall be punished with a sentence of imprisonment of 3 to 10 years.

2. If the purpose of the conduct described in the preceding paragraph is that indicated in article 293º, the offender shall be punished with a sentence of imprisonment of up to 2 years or with a fine.

###### Artigo 295º

Embezzlement

1. An official who unlawfully appropriates for his own benefit or that of another person, money or movable property, public or private, that is delivered to him, is in his possession or is accessible to him by reason of his functions, shall be punished with a sentence of imprisonment of 3 to 10 years, if a more severe penalty is not applicable by virtue of another legal provision.

2. If the official lends, pawns or, in any way, encumbers the valuables or objects referred to in the preceding paragraph, he shall be punished with a sentence of imprisonment of up to 3 years or a fine, if a more severe penalty is not applicable by virtue of another legal provision.

3. If the valuables or objects referred to in the preceding paragraphs exceed 5,000 US dollars, the penalties shall be, respectively, imprisonment of 4 to 12 years or 2 to 5 years.

4. If the valuables or objects referred to in paragraph 1 are of a value less than 50 US dollars, the offender shall be punished with a sentence of imprisonment of up to 3 years or with a fine.

###### Artigo 296º

Embezzlement of use

An official who makes use of, or permits another person to use, for purposes other than those for which they are intended, vehicles or other movable things of significant value, that are delivered to him, are in his possession or are accessible to him by reason of his functions, in order to obtain for himself or for a third party an unlawful benefit or to cause harm to someone, shall be punished with imprisonment of up to 2 years, if those things are in his possession or are accessible to him by reason of his functions.

###### Artigo 297º

Abuse of power

An official who abuses powers or violates duties inherent to his functions, with the intention of obtaining for himself or for a third party an unlawful benefit or causing harm to another person, shall be punished with a sentence of imprisonment of 1 to 4 years, if a more severe penalty is not applicable by virtue of another legal provision.

###### Artigo 298º

Abusive use of public force

An official who, having competence to use, requisition or order the deployment of public force, does so to prevent the execution of the law, a regular court order or a lawful order of a public authority, shall be punished with a sentence of imprisonment of up to 3 years, if a more severe penalty is not applicable by virtue of another legal provision.

###### Artigo 299º

Economic participation in business

1. An official who, by reason of the exercise of public office, must intervene in a contract or other operation or activity, and takes advantage of that condition to obtain for himself or for a third party, directly or through an intermediary, a patrimonial advantage, or, in any other way, unlawful economic participation and thereby harms the public interests that it is his duty to administer, supervise, defend or realize, shall be punished with a sentence of imprisonment of 2 to 8 years.

2. If the preceding conduct results in losses to the State exceeding 10,000 US dollars, the penalty of imprisonment shall be 3 to 15 years.

###### Artigo 300º

Refusal to cooperate

An official who, having been lawfully requested by the competent authority to provide due cooperation for the administration of justice or any other public service, and refuses to provide it or without justified reason fails to provide it, shall be punished with a sentence of imprisonment of up to 3 years or with a fine.

###### Artigo 301º

Aggravation

1. When the crimes referred to in this Title are committed by holders of political office or by magistrates, the prescribed penalties shall be increased by one third of their maximum limit.

2. The non-criminal and procedural effects relating to the situations referred to in the preceding paragraph shall be established in special legislation.

###### Artigo 302º

Concept of public official

1. For the purposes of criminal law, the term public official encompasses:

- a) The civil servant;
- b) The administrative agent;
- c) Members of the armed forces and police;
- d) Anyone who, even provisionally or temporarily, with or without remuneration, voluntarily or mandatorily, has been called to perform or participate in the performance of an activity comprised in the administrative or jurisdictional public function, or, under the same circumstances, performs functions in public utility organizations or participates in them.
- e) The foreign public official who holds a legislative, executive, administrative or judicial office of a foreign country, already designated or installed, or person who exercises a public function for a foreign country, including in a public agency or public company.
- f) The official of a public international organization whom such organization has authorized to act on its behalf
  - 2. For criminal purposes, the provisions of this title apply to those who perform political, governmental or legislative functions.

#### TITLE VII

CRIMES OF FALSIFICATION

##### CHAPTER I

FALSIFICATION OF DOCUMENTS

###### Artigo 303º

Falsification of document or technical notation

1. Whoever, with intent to cause harm to another person or to the State, or to obtain for himself or for another person, illegitimate benefit:

- a) Manufactures false document or technical notation, falsifies or alters document or abuses another person's signature to create false document;
- b) Falsely inserts in document or technical notation legally relevant fact;
- c) Falsely attests, based on professional, technical or scientific knowledge, about the physical or mental state or quality of persons, animals or things;
- d) Uses any of the documents or technical notations referred to in the preceding paragraphs, manufactured or falsified or issued by another; is punished with imprisonment up to 3 years or fine.
  - 2. Equivalent to falsification of technical notation is the disruptive action on technical or automatic devices by means of which the results of the notation are influenced.
  - 3. The attempt is punishable.

###### Artigo 304º

Aggravated falsification

1. If the facts referred to in number 1 of the previous article relate to authentic document or with equal force, to sealed will, to postal order, to bill of exchange, to check, other commercial documents transferable by endorsement or to technical notation relating to the identification, in part or in whole, of motor vehicles, aircraft or boats, the agent is punished with imprisonment from 2 to 6 years.

2. If the facts described in the previous number or in number 1 of artigo 309º are committed by a public official in the exercise of his functions, the penalty is from 2 to 8 years of imprisonment.

###### Artigo 305º

Falsification of public document

The public official who, in the exercise of his functions, with intent to cause harm to another person or to the State, or to obtain for himself or for another person illegitimate benefit:

- a) Omits fact that the document to which the law attributes public faith is intended to certify or authenticate; or
- b) Inserts act or document in protocol, registry or official book without complying with legal formalities; is punished with imprisonment from 2 to 6 years.

###### Artigo 306º

Use of another person's identification document

Whoever, with intent to cause harm to another person or to the State, uses identification document of which another person is the holder, is punished with imprisonment up to 1 year or fine.

##### CHAPTER II

COUNTERFEITING OF CURRENCY

###### Artigo 307º

Counterfeiting of currency

1. Whoever commits counterfeiting of currency or depreciates legitimate metallic currency, with intent to put it into circulation as genuine, is punished with imprisonment from 3 to 10 years.

2. If the agent, in addition to committing the facts described in the previous number, actually puts the currency into circulation, the penalty is increased by one third in the maximum limit.

3. Whoever, by agreement with the counterfeiter, exposes for sale, puts into circulation or by any other means disseminates the currency, is punished with imprisonment from 3 to 10 years.

###### Artigo 308º

Passing of false currency

Whoever, outside the cases referred to in the previous article, acquires to put into circulation or actually puts into circulation, sells or by any means disseminates counterfeit or depreciated currency, as if it were genuine, is punished with imprisonment from 2 to 6 years.

###### Artigo 309º

Counterfeiting of stamped or embossed securities

1. Whoever, to sell them, use them or by any other means put them into circulation as legitimate, commits counterfeiting or falsification of stamped or embossed securities whose manufacture and supply belongs exclusively to the Timorese State, is punished with imprisonment from 2 to 8 years.

2. Whoever commits the described facts in relation to postage stamps or any other means of postal franking in use by the Post Office of Timor-Leste is punished with imprisonment up to 3 years or fine.

3. The use of the falsified products referred to in the previous numbers, knowing them to be falsified, is punished with imprisonment up to 2 years or fine.

4. The attempt is punishable.

###### Artigo 310º

Counterfeiting of seals, stamps, marks or chancery stamps

1. Whoever, with intent to use them as authentic or intact, acquires, counterfeits or falsifies seals, stamps, marks or chancery stamps of any authority or public office is punished with imprisonment from 2 to 6 years.

2. Whoever uses the objects referred to in the previous number knowing them to be falsified or without authorization from the rightful person, to cause harm to another person or to the State, is punished with imprisonment up to 3 years or fine.

3. If the person using said objects is the falsifier himself, the penalty of number 1 is increased by one third in the maximum limit.

4. The attempt to commit the facts described in number 2 is punishable.

###### Artigo 311º

Weights and measures

1. Whoever, with intent to harm another person or the State, falsifies or by any other means alters or uses, after such acts are committed, weights, measures, scales or other measuring instruments, is punished with imprisonment up to 3 years or fine.

2. The attempt is punishable.

###### Artigo 312º

Seizure and forfeiture

Counterfeit, falsified or altered currencies, and equivalent objects, as well as weights, measures or any and all instruments intended for the commission of the crimes provided for in this Title, are seized and put out of use or destroyed.

#### TITLE VIII

CRIMES AGAINST THE ECONOMY

###### Artigo 313º

Money laundering

1. Whoever, knowing that the goods or products come from the practice of crimes, in any form of participation, terrorism, trafficking in weapons or nuclear products, trafficking or sale of persons, pornography involving minors, corruption, fraud or extortion, tax fraud, trafficking in protected species and trafficking in human organs or tissues or other serious crimes, whose minimum limit of the sentence is more than 4 years of imprisonment:

- a) Converts, transfers, assists or facilitates any conversion or transfer operation of such goods or products, in whole or in part, directly or indirectly, with the purpose of concealing or disguising their illicit origin or helping a person involved in the practice of any of these offences to evade the legal consequences of their acts; or
- b) Conceals or disguises the true nature, origin, location, disposition, movement or properties of such goods or products or rights related to them;
- c) Acquires or receives such advantages in any capacity or uses or holds or keeps them, is punished with a prison sentence of 4 to 12 years.
  - 2. The punishment of the acts provided for in paragraphs a) to c) of the previous number takes place even if the facts that constitute the underlying crime were committed outside the national territory or even if the place of commission of the act or the identity of the perpetrators is unknown.
  - 3. The knowledge, intention or purpose, required as constituent elements of the crime may be recognised from actual and concrete factual circumstances.
  - 4. For the demonstration and proof of the illicit origin of the income, prior conviction of the agent for the practice of the underlying crime is not necessary.
  - 5. The underlying offence includes crimes committed outside the national territory, when the acts are constituted as a crime in the state where they were committed and within the territory of Timor-Leste.
  - 6. The crime of money laundering does not apply to persons who commit the underlying crime.
  - 7. The attempt of money laundering is punishable and may be subject to a reduction of sentence under general terms.
  - 8. The punishment provided for the practice of the typical illicit acts described in paragraphs a) to c) of number 1 must not exceed the maximum limit provided for the corresponding underlying offences.

###### Artigo 314º

Tax fraud

1. Whoever, in order not to pay or allow a third party not to pay, totally or partially, any tax, fee or other pecuniary tax obligation owed to the State, by:

- a) Not declaring the facts subject to taxation or those necessary for its settlement;
- b) Incorrectly declaring the facts on which the taxation is based; or
- c) Preventing by any means or withholding the elements necessary for a correct inspection of the activity or facts subject to taxation. is punished with a prison sentence of 2 to 6 years.
  - 2. If the amount owed and not paid is more than 5,000 US dollars, the agent is punished with a prison sentence of 2 to 8 years.

###### Artigo 315º

Illicit import and export of goods or merchandise

1. Whoever exports or imports, without a licence, goods or merchandise whose export or import, by legal determination, is dependent on a licence from any entity, or without passing through customs, is punished with imprisonment up to 3 years or a fine.

2. If the acts are committed negligently the sentence is imprisonment up to 1 year or a fine.

###### Artigo 316º

Smuggling

1. Whoever imports or exports goods or merchandise without conducting them through customs or other mandatory passage controls for entry or exit of goods or merchandise from the territory of Timor-Leste, is punished with a prison sentence of 2 to 6 years or a fine.

2. If the value of the goods or merchandise is more than 10,000 US dollars or if the agent commits the acts described in the previous number habitually, the sentence is imprisonment of 2 to 8 years or a fine.

3. If the conduct referred to in the previous numbers concerns goods or merchandise subject to licence or whose import or export is prohibited, the sentences referred to in the previous numbers are aggravated by one third in their limits.

###### Artigo 317º

Customs evasion

1. Whoever imports or exports goods or merchandise, evading totally or partially, the payment of duties or customs fees owed for the entry or exit of those is punished with a prison sentence of 1 to 4 years or a fine.

2. If the value of the goods or merchandise is more than 10,000 US dollars or if the agent commits the acts described in the previous number habitually, the sentence is imprisonment of 2 to 6 years or a fine.

###### Artigo 318º

Exemption from penalty

The agent of the practice of the acts described in the previous articles may be exempt from penalty whenever they voluntarily pay the duties or customs fees that are to be settled and the conduct is occasional.

###### Artigo 319º

Irregular administration of public funds

1. Whoever gives public funds an application different from that legally established, is punished with imprisonment up to 2 years or a fine.

2. If the destination of the irregular application is not made for a public purpose, this being the legally established purpose, the sentence is imprisonment of 2 to 6 years.

###### Artigo 320º

Disobedience to requisition of goods

1. Whoever does not comply with the requisition, ordered by the Government, of goods considered essential for the supply of economic activities or public consumption, is punished with a prison sentence of 1 to 4 years or a fine.

2. Negligent conduct is punished with a prison sentence up to 1 year or a fine.

###### Artigo 321º

Destruction of goods relevant to the economy

1. Whoever by any means, destroys, damages or renders unusable their own goods of relevant interest to the national economy or in any other way removes them from compliance with legal duties imposed by reason of the national economy is punished with a prison sentence up to 3 years or a fine.

2. Negligent conduct is punished with imprisonment up to 1 year or a fine.

###### Artigo 322º

Illicit exploitation of gambling

1. Whoever, in any way, operates games of fortune or chance outside legally authorised locations and without proper legal authorisation, or ensures winning through error, deception or use of any equipment, is punished with a prison sentence up to 3 years or with a fine.

2. Games of fortune or chance are those whose result is uncertain because it is based exclusively or fundamentally on luck.

###### Article 323º

Disturbance of public act

Whoever, with the intention of preventing or prejudicing the results of a judicial auction or against public auction or competition, succeeds, by means of gift, promise, violence or threat, in causing someone not to bid or not to compete, or that, even while bidding and acquiring, does so under conditions of lack of freedom in the practice of those acts, shall be punished with imprisonment of up to 3 years or a fine. Law 13/2008, of 13 October

Legislative authorization in criminal matters

The constitutional mechanism of legislative authorization is provided for in article 96, no. 1, paragraph a) of the Constitution of the Democratic Republic of Timor-Leste. The choice of this mechanism, which the Constitution provides for, enables greater speed in the legislative process, with Parliament defining the broad lines of legislative policy that should inform the final enactment, leaving to the Government the harmonization of aspects of legal technique, an intervention subordinate to the directives emanating from the National Parliament. The separation of powers attributed to the various constitutional bodies is observed with regard to the exercise of legislative power.

The present legislative authorization law establishes the content and scope in terms that guarantee respect for the fundamental rights, freedoms and guarantees of citizens, within the scope of Criminal Law. Furthermore, the proposed solutions take into account the general principles of criminal law enshrined in international legal instruments, already ratified by Timor-Leste, as well as the social and cultural context of the country.

It should also be noted that the content of the legislative authorization now granted is articulated with the Code of Criminal Procedure and other complementary legislation that has meanwhile been approved and is in force.

The approval has as a future consequence the abandonment of the subsidiary application in Timor-Leste of the Penal Code of Indonesia, also requiring the revocation or harmonization of legislative enactments after 20 May 2002, which contain rules relating to the definition of crimes, penalties or security measures. Thus, the National Parliament decrees, pursuant to articles 95, no. 1 and 96 of the Constitution of the Republic, to have effect as law, the following:

###### Article 1º

Object

Authorization is granted to the Government to approve the Penal Code and revoke the legislation in force in this matter, namely with regard to the definition of crimes, penalties, security measures and their respective prerequisites.

###### Article 2

Meaning and Scope

1. The Penal Code drafted under this legislative authorization law shall observe the constitutional principles and norms and the norms contained in international instruments that, in this matter, bind the Timorese State.

2. The authorization has the following meaning and scope:

- a) Construction of a criminal legal system aimed at the protection of legal interests essential to life in Timorese society and the reintegration of offenders therein;
- b) Establish the principle of legality and the prohibition of analogy with regard to the application of criminal law, although with the possibility of applying law subsequent to the commission of the crime whenever it proves more favorable in concrete terms;
- c) Stipulate that exceptional or temporary laws apply to acts committed during their validity even if judged after the period of validity;
- d) Establish norms that define the place and time of commission of the crime;
- e) Establish the principle of territoriality for the application of Timorese criminal law, individualizing the restrictions to such principle and the cases of application to situations occurring outside Timorese territory;
- f) Regulate the general prerequisites of crime, equating omission to action, unless the intention of the law is otherwise, whenever the legal type of crime comprises a certain result and the agent has a duty to prevent it;
- g) Make objective imputation dependent on the existence of a causal nexus between the conduct and the typical result;
- h) Objective imputation shall depend on the verification of intent or negligence, defining the types of intent (direct, necessary and eventual) and the modalities of negligence (conscious and unconscious);
- i) Provide that criminal liability for crimes provided for in the Penal Code is non-transferable and has as sole agents natural persons, admitting situations of liability for acting on behalf of others and that special legislation may establish criminal liability of legal persons;
- j) Establish the non-imputability of minors under 16 years of age and the requirements for non-imputability by reason of mental abnormality;
- k) Admit the possibility of aggravation of the penalty whenever the agent's liability, at least for negligence, must encompass a result not included in the type;
- l) Establish error as to circumstances as excluding intent and error as to unlawfulness as relevant to exclude guilt;
- m) As to the forms of crime, establish as a principle the non-punishability of preparatory acts, and the rule of punishment of attempt in intentional crimes punishable with more than 3 years of imprisonment;
- n) Define the concept of attempt, the measure of its punishment, cases of impossible attempt, the relevance of voluntary desistance and subsequent repentance both in individual actions and in cases of complicity;
- o) Establish the forms of authorship, instigation or complicity and define the liability of participants in guilt and unlawfulness;
- p) Provide for and define the prerequisites of situations of concurrence of crimes, concurrence of sanctions, concurrence of crime with other offense and concurrence of norms;
- q) Establish the rules for punishment of concurrence of crimes, even when of supervening knowledge, admitting the possibility that the cumulation may reach 30 years of imprisonment and 600 days of fine;
- r) Define continued crime and the rules for its punishment with the penalty applicable to the most serious conduct;
- s) Establish the causes of exclusion of unlawfulness and guilt, systematically autonomizing one and the other and defining the respective prerequisites for application;
- t) Introduce a title relating to circumstances that, not forming part of the legal type, assume relevance in determining the criminal liability of the agent;
- u) Proceed with the enumeration of aggravating circumstances of a general nature and the enumeration of mitigating circumstances;
- v) Provide for a general clause of extraordinary mitigation beyond the special cases provided for in the law and delimit the respective consequences in determining the penal framework, through a model of degrees of mitigation;
- w) As general modifying aggravating circumstances, establish recidivism and criminal habituality, providing for the respective prerequisites and consequences in the domain of the penalty;
- x) Construct a system of penalties and security measures whose difference is based on the capacity or not of guilt of the agent of the act; and in the first case the concrete penalty may never exceed the measure of guilt and in the second must be based on the dangerousness of the agent;
- y) Enunciate the determining principles in the choice of penalty and security measure as well as the effects thereof and the counting of time of deprivation of liberty before conviction;
- z) With regard to the execution of penalties and custodial security measures, provide for the possibility of benefit of conditional release and the regime for probation, always with the consent of the person concerned and with other requirements to be established in complementary legislation that may provide for the conditions of execution of the prison sentence, the rights and duties of prisoners and the existence of open regimes; aa) Create a system of criminal sanctions for those imputable based on the following main penalties: imprisonment, fine, community service work and admonishment; bb) The penalty of imprisonment with a minimum duration of 30 days and maximum of 30 years, with the possibility of being suspended in its execution whenever it is not applied in a measure exceeding 3 years and the requirements of prevention of future crimes do not require its fulfillment, and may or may not be conditioned on compliance with some duties or/and subject to monitoring by reintegration services during the suspension period;
  - cc) Regulate the possibility of modification of the suspension regime initially applied and its revocation if during the suspension period the convicted person commits an intentional crime for which he is convicted with a prison sentence or in cases of intentional non-compliance with the duties to which he is subject; dd) Establish a regime of substitution of prison sentences applied in a measure not exceeding 12 months by fine or community service work, according to the circumstances, with the duty of the court to justify the non-substitution when, being able to, it does not use such measure; ee) Not make alternative imprisonment correspond to the substitute fine and, in case of unjustified non-payment thereof, impose the fulfillment of the imprisonment initially applied; ff) Stipulate that the fine penalty shall always be fixed in time, at a minimum of 10 days and maximum of 360 days, in principle, and determine that each day of fine corresponds to an amount to be fixed between half a dollar and 200 dollars, to be fixed according to the economic and financial situation of the convicted person; gg) Whenever a fine penalty is directly applied, stipulate the duty to make alternative imprisonment correspond to it for the corresponding time reduced to 2/3, as well as the possibility of payment of the fine in installments, its reduction or exemption in cases of great supervening difficulty or impossibility of compliance therewith; hh) Provide for the possibility that the penalty of community service work may be a substitute for imprisonment or fine in situations of minor and medium criminality and establish some of the conditions for its application and execution in coordination with reintegration services, as well as cases of exemption or reduction, leaving to complementary law the regulation of other conditions of application and operation of this penalty;
  - ii) Establish the penalty of admonishment for cases not punishable with abstract imprisonment exceeding 3 years provided that, cumulatively, there is reparation of the damage, the agent is a first-time offender and the needs of prevention and rehabilitation are satisfied with admonishment; jj) Establish a system of accessory penalties, making certain crimes correspond to the prohibition of the exercise of rights and professions, namely the temporary suspension of public functions, prohibition of the exercise of function, prohibition of driving, revocation of gun license and expulsion of foreigners; such accessory penalties shall not operate automatically, depending on the prior application of a main penalty and their duration variable with the measure of guilt; kk) To those declared non-imputable by virtue of mental abnormality, establish the possibility of application of security measures, of limited temporal duration, namely of internment whenever reasons of dangerousness so advise and the obligation thereof to cease when the state that legitimized it ends, admitting that in the case of foreigners it may be replaced by expulsion;
  - ll) Also provide for the existence of measures of professional interdiction, revocation of license and prohibition of driving for those non-imputable by reason of mental abnormality; mm) Create an autonomous chapter relating to the matter of determination of the concrete penalty, both imprisonment and fine, enunciating the general principles and the operations necessary for its choice and quantification; nn) Stipulate that compensation for losses and damages arising from the commission of a crime, as to the prerequisites and calculation, is governed by the norms of civil law but, unless otherwise declared by the injured party, shall be officially awarded by the court in the criminal proceedings and that the resulting credit is privileged; oo) Determine the conditions for the loss of objects and advantages resulting from a crime to be decreed by the court; pp) Define for purposes of the exercise of the right of complaint crimes as public or semi-public, identify the holders of that right, the time limit for its exercise and the conditions of renunciation or waiver thereof; qq) With regard to the extinction of criminal liability, fix the periods of prescription of criminal proceedings taking into account the different frameworks of the type of crimes, the form of counting and the causes of suspension of prescription; rr) Fix the periods for the prescription of penalties, security measures and accessory penalties, as well as situations of suspension thereof; ss) Regulate other cases of extinction of liability such as the death of the agent, amnesty, general pardon and pardon; tt) Establish the imprescriptibility of criminal proceedings and penalties for the commission of crimes of war, against peace, humanity and freedom; uu) Organize the special part of the Penal Code, Book Two, grouping the types of crimes according to the protected legal interests, creating Titles and Chapters as homogeneous as possible;
  - vv) In Title I, group the illicit acts concerning crimes of Genocide and against Humanity, crimes of War, including the prohibitive conducts of certain methods and means referenced in international law, war crimes against property and against goods protected by distinctive insignia or emblems or other rights and, also in this Title, the so-called crimes against Peace and Freedom, namely prohibiting incitement to war, conducts of racial or religious discrimination, trafficking and slavery of persons and establishing a legal type of crime that encompasses situations of terrorism; ww) Also in Title I of the Special Part, the future Penal Code of Timor-Leste must take into account the international obligations assumed by the Country in subscribing to and ratifying the Statute of the International Criminal Court;
  - xx) In Title II of the Special Part of the Code, eminently personal legal interests shall be protected, highlighting life, physical integrity, personal freedom, honor and the protection of private life; yy) Structure the protection of the legal interest of life through the threat of differentiated penal frameworks according to the type of guilt of the agent (intent and negligence) and the qualifying circumstances of the greater or lesser degree of censurability, namely according to the motives, the quality of the victim and the means and methods used to carry out the criminal design; zz) Establish the protection of uterine life; provide for the privileging of infanticidal conducts by the parturient to restrictive cases occurring during childbirth, due to the disturbing influence thereof; criminalize incitement to suicide and situations of intentional abandonment or exposure of others that place their life in danger; aaa) Define a set of incriminating norms that prohibit behaviors violating the physical integrity of another person, graduating the abstract penal framework based on the degree of unlawfulness and guilt of the agent manifested in the seriousness of the injury, in the type of guilt and in the possibility or not of a certain result that exceeds the formulated criminal design still supporting imputation, at least for negligence, what the doctrine calls aggravation by result; bbb) Autonomize the incrimination of offenses by means of poisonous substances, reciprocal offenses and participation in brawl with two or more persons;
  - ccc) Lastly, still within the scope of bodily offenses, implement two legal types of crime that specifically provide for the punishment of mistreatment of minors or incapacitated persons and of spouses or those cohabiting in an analogous situation; ddd) In the chapter on the protection of personal freedom, criminalize conducts traditionally and at the level of comparative law integrating the types of crime of threats, coercion, kidnapping, abduction and other acts violating individual freedom and, specifically, create the type of illicit act relating to the sale of persons and the omission of denunciation of situations likely to constitute the crimes in question; eee) Establish a chapter relating to sexual criminality highlighting the legal interest of freedom in this domain as determinant of the conducts to be criminalized, protecting the youngest from acts for which they are not yet prepared to consciously decide in freedom, incriminate conducts revealing sexual exploitation and the most common forms of sexual abuse whether by fraud or by reason of age; fff) In a very particular way, incriminate the practices of sexual prostitution, child pornography and any acts of a sexual nature with minors under twelve years of age; ggg) Introduce the criminalization of violation of secrecy, violation of domicile and other places and violation of correspondence and telecommunications; hhh) Group in a single title a set of crimes that protect legal interests relating to public peace and tranquility, State security, life in society, against public authority and in the context of electoral activities;
  - iii) With regard to the domain of conducts capable of violating public peace and tranquility, prevent the emergence of criminal associations, public attitudes of instigation to the commission of crimes, participation in public riots, behaviors that prevent the exercise of political rights by citizens, disobedience to the lawful order of dispersal, criminalize practices constituting influence peddling and punish the abuse of public signs or uniforms; jjj) Within the scope of internal State security, provide for incriminating norms for behaviors likely to put it at risk in a more significant way, namely establishing the legal type of crime of treason to the Homeland, collaboration with armed forces enemy to the Timorese State, sabotage against national defense and campaign against the effort for peace in Timor-Leste, attempt against the Head of State or other actions against constitutional bodies or conducts that pursue as an end the alteration of the Rule of Law, diplomatic infidelity, violation of State secrecy and outrage to Timorese national symbols; kkk) Criminalize fraudulent behaviors in electoral activities regardless of who their agent is and the phase of the electoral process, cases of obstruction to the exercise of candidacy or its illicit exercise, situations of electoral propaganda in violation of the respective legal framework and actions that disturb the normal conduct of the electoral act;
  - lll) Life in modern societies develops in constant contact with conducts potentially generating dangers more or less close to concretization and likely to violate interests whose integrity it is important to safeguard, such as the activity of driving motor vehicles, use and possession of weapons and certain activities with relevance in the domain of citizen and public health, and some types of crimes in this matter should be provided for; mmm) In the same way, behaviors capable of violating religious values established in Timorese society should be criminalized, failure to comply with maintenance obligations and others in the domain of custody of minors, cases of social solidarity arising from situations of calamity, disaster or mandatory for certain professional activities; nnn) Criminally sanction conducts that are unsustainably harmful to the environment, namely to fauna, flora, natural habitats and resources of the Timorese subsoil; ooo) Criminally sanction conducts that attack the exercise of public authority, namely by means of acts of disobedience, obstruction, destruction, resistance or usurpation of public offices; ppp) With regard to the exercise of the action of execution of penalties or custodial security measures, criminalize behaviors that translate into removal of prisoners, escape, assistance thereto by an official and mutiny of inmates; qqq) As a form of protection of patrimonial legal interests, establish two chapters grouping crimes against property in one and crimes against patrimony in general in another; rrr) Construct a normative system protecting property based on the legal types of crime most common in the various criminal legislations such as theft, robbery, breach of trust and damage, structuring these types into simple and aggravated, considering the circumstances of value combined with the nature of the thing appropriated, the means and forms of action, violence, threats or coercion or any others likely to significantly increase guilt or unlawfulness; sss) Autonomize, also with regard to property, the norms typifying theft of use of vehicles, cases of violence after subtraction, usurpation of real property and alteration of landmarks, maintaining the incrimination of involuntary damage and defining the types of prohibited burning and arson as types of crime specifically of special damage by reason of the means and of danger in view of the consequences normally associated therewith; ttt) Protect patrimony in general not only with the most usual incriminations in this matter such as fraud, extortion, receiving and bankruptcy or insolvency but, above all, bearing in mind the needs arising from new technologies in commerce and the responsibilities to be demanded of those who, managing assets or patrimony of others, do not conduct themselves adequately in that profession of administration, we refer to crimes of computer fraud and forms of harmful and abusive administration;
