- z) Regarding the execution of penalties and measures depriving liberty, provide for the possibility of granting conditional release and probation regime, when, with the consent of the convicted person and with other requirements to be written in complementary legislation that will provide for conditions for the execution of imprisonment, the rights and obligations of prisoners and also open regime;
- aa) Establish a system of criminal sanctions for imputable persons based on these principal penalties: imprisonment, fine, community service and admonishment;
- bb) Imprisonment penalty with its minimum duration of 30 days and maximum of 30 years, with the possibility of suspending its execution when the penalty applied is not longer than 3 years and the fulfillment of that penalty is not necessary to prevent future crime, with or without conditions to fulfill any duty and/or enter into accompaniment for reintegration during the suspension period;
- cc) Regulate the possibility of modifying the suspension regime applied anew and the revocation of suspension when during the suspension period the convicted person practices an intentional crime giving rise to a penalty of imprisonment or in cases where there is intentional non-fulfillment of the obligation that falls upon him;
- dd) Consecrate a regime regarding the substitution of applied imprisonment penalty not longer than 12 months with a fine or community service, according to each circumstance, with an obligation for the court to justify non-substitution in situations where, although possible, this measure is not applied;
- ee) Not give correspondence between alternative imprisonment and substitute fine and, when there is unjustified non-payment of that fine, give an obligation to apply the imprisonment applied anew;
- ff) Stipulate that the fine penalty must be fixed by time, with its minimum at 10 days and its maximum at 360 days, as a rule, and determine that each day of fine shall correspond to an amount, with its minimum at fifty cents and its maximum at 200 dollars, which shall be fixed according to the economic and financial situation of the convicted person;
- gg) When directly applying a fine penalty, stipulate the obligation to give correspondence to that fine with alternative imprisonment during the corresponding time reduced by 2/3, and also the possibility of paying the fine with installments, reduction or exemption of that fine in cases where there is supervening great difficulty or impossibility of paying that fine;
- hh) Provide for the possibility of placing a community service penalty in place of an imprisonment penalty or a fine penalty in situations regarding petty criminality and medium criminality and establish conditions for its application and execution according to coordination with reintegration services, and also cases regarding exemption or reduction, and leave to complementary law to regulate other conditions regarding the application and functioning of this penalty;
- ii) Consecrate admonishment penalty for cases not punishable with abstract imprisonment penalty longer than 3 years, namely, cumulatively, there is reparation for damage, the agent is a primary delinquent and admonishment is sufficient to make prevention and recovery;
- jj) Establish a system of accessory penalties, in which, because of certain crimes, the exercise of rights and professions will be prohibited, namely, temporary suspension from public office, prohibition to exercise office, prohibition on driving, revocation of license to carry a weapon and expulsion of foreigners; these accessory penalties do not function automatically but apply only when the principal penalty applies and their measure varies according to the measure of culpability;
- kk) Consecrate for those already declared non-imputable due to psychic anomaly the possibility of applying security measures, with limited temporal duration, namely internment when dangerousness counsels and also consecrate that the obligation to apply that measure ends when the state giving rise to that measure ends, and for foreigners expulsion may be placed in place of that measure;
- ll) Provide also for non-imputables due to psychic anomaly the measures of professional interdiction, revocation of license and prohibition on driving;
- mm) Establish an autonomous chapter regarding the determination of the concrete penalty, for imprisonment and for fine, indicating general principles and necessary operations for the choice and quantification of that penalty;
- nn) Establish that civil law regulates compensation for loss and damage arising from the practice of crime, and also its prerequisites and calculation, but, except for another declaration by the injured party, the court shall award compensation ex officio and the credit arising from it shall have privilege;
- oo) Determine conditions giving rise to the court declaring forfeited any object or advantage arising from crime;
- pp) Define, as a criterion to exercise the right to complaint, various crimes as public or semi-public, identify the holder of that right, the deadline to exercise that right and conditions for the renunciation and desistance of that right;
- qq) Regarding the extinction of criminal responsibility, fix the deadline for the prescription of criminal proceedings, following the various frameworks of these various crimes, how it is counted and causes for the suspension of prescription;
- rr) Fix the deadline for the prescription of penalties, security measures and accessory penalties, and also situations giving rise to the suspension of prescription;
- ss) Regulate other extinctions of responsibility, because of the agent's death, because of amnesty, because of generic pardon and because of pardon;
- tt) Consecrate imprescriptibility for criminal proceedings and penalties because of war crimes, against peace, humanity and freedom;
- uu) Organize the special part of the Penal Code, book II, in which the various types of crime are included according to the legal good protected in homogeneous titles and chapters as far as possible;
- vv) In title I include illicit acts regarding crimes of genocide and against humanity, war crimes, including prohibited conducts using methods and means referred to in international law, war crimes against property and against goods protected with distinctive insignia or emblem or other rights, and, also in this title, crimes called crimes against peace and freedom, namely prohibit incitement to war, racially or religiously discriminatory conduct, trafficking and enslavement of persons and also consecrate a type of crime regarding terrorism;
- ww) In title I of the general part, Timor-Leste's future Penal Code must also pay attention to the international obligations that the Country has already assumed when subscribing to and ratifying the Statute of the International Criminal Court;
- xx) In title II of the special part the eminently personal legal goods shall be protected, such as life, physical integrity, personal freedom, honour and protection of private life;
- yy) Structure the protection of the legal good life with provision for various penal frameworks according to what type of culpability the agent has (intent and negligence) and circumstances that qualify the degree of censurability as lesser or greater, namely according to the motive, the quality of the victim and the means and method used to realize the criminal design;
- zz) Consecrate protection for uterine life; provide as privileged the conduct of the infanticidal parturient in limited cases occurring during childbirth because of the disturbing influence of childbirth; criminalize incitement to suicide and intentional abandonment or exposure of another person causing danger to life;
- aaa) Define a broad incriminating norm that prohibits conduct violating the physical integrity of another person, with the graduation of the abstract penal framework according to the agent's unlawfulness and culpability already shown in the gravity of the injury, according to the type of culpability and according to whether the result that went beyond the formulated criminal design may or may not give rise to imputation, at least for negligence - what doctrine calls aggravation because of result;
- bbb) Autonomize incrimination for offence with poisonous substance, reciprocal offence and participation in a fight with two or more persons;
- ccc) In the context of bodily offence, two legal types of crime shall be implemented providing for punishment for ill-treatment of minors or incapacitated persons and of spouse or person living as husband and wife;
- ddd) In the chapter regarding personal freedom, conduct that, traditionally and according to comparative law, integrates the types of crime of threat, coercion, kidnapping, abduction, and other acts violating individual freedom and, specifically, create a typical illicit act regarding human trafficking and omission of denunciation regarding situations that may constitute these crimes;
- eee) Consecrate a chapter on sexual criminality, with emphasis on the legal good of freedom in this area as determinant for the conduct to be criminalized, in order to protect those who are still younger from acts for which they do not yet have preparation to, according to their conscience, decide with freedom, incriminate conduct constituting the most common forms of sexual exploitation and sexual abuse, such as with fraud or because of age;
- fff) Specifically incriminate practices constituting sexual prostitution, child pornography and other sexual acts with minors under the age of [number missing];
- ggg) Include criminalization for violation of secrecy, violation of domicile and other places and violation of correspondence and telecommunications;
- hhh) Put together in a single title crimes protecting legal goods regarding public peace and tranquility, State security, life in society, against public authority and in the context of electoral activity;
- iii) Regarding conduct that may violate public peace and tranquility, prevent criminal association, public incitement to practice crime, participation in public riot, conduct preventing citizens from exercising political rights, disobedience to legitimate order to disperse, criminalize practices constituting trafficking in influence and also punish abuse of public signs or uniforms;
- jjj) Regarding the State's internal security, provide for incriminating norms for conduct that may truly cause danger to that security, namely consecrate as legal types of crime treason against the homeland, collaboration with the enemy armed forces of the Timorese State, sabotage of national defense and campaign against peace efforts in Timor-Leste, attack against the Head of State or other constitutional organs or conduct seeking to alter the State according to law, diplomatic infidelity, violation of State secrecy and outrage to Timorese national symbols;
- kkk) Criminalize fraudulent conduct in electoral activity independently of its agent and phase of the electoral process, obstruction to exercise candidacy or exercise of illegal candidacy, electoral propaganda against the respective legal framework and actions disturbing the normal development of the electoral act;
- lll) Life in modern society develops together with conduct that may give rise to danger with more or less close concretization and may violate goods whose integrity must be guaranteed, such as driving a motor vehicle, using or carrying weapons and activity with relevance in the health of citizens and the public; therefore certain types of crime regarding this matter must be provided for;
- mmm) Thus also conduct violating religious values implanted in Timorese society must be criminalized, non-fulfillment of maintenance obligation and other obligations regarding the custody of minors, for solidarity in calamity or disaster or obligations falling on certain professional activities;
- nnn) Criminally sanction unsustainable harmful conduct against the environment, namely fauna, flora, natural habitat and resources in the Timorese subsoil;
- ooo) Criminally sanction conduct against the exercise of public authority, namely with acts constituting disobedience, obstruction, destruction, resistance or usurpation of public office;
- ppp) Regarding the executory action of penalties and security measures that remove freedom, criminalize conduct constituting removal of a prisoner, evasion, assistance by an official to evasion and riot of prisoners;
- qqq) To protect patrimonial legal goods, consecrate in one chapter crimes against property and in another chapter crimes against patrimony;
- rrr) Establish a normative system protecting property based on the legal types of crime used for a long time in other penal legislation, such as theft, robbery, breach of trust and damage, and structure these types as simple and aggravated, according to circumstances such as the value and nature of the thing appropriated, the means used, how the acting is, violence, threat or coercion or other circumstances that may significantly increase culpability and unlawfulness;
- sss) Regarding property, also autonomize the norm typifying theft for use of vehicle, violence following subtraction, usurpation of immovable property and alteration of boundary marks, also incriminate involuntary damage and define prohibited burning and types of arson as typical crimes regarding special damage because of the means and danger represented with the consequences arising from these conducts;
- ttt) Protect patrimony in general with these common incriminations, such as fraud, extortion, receiving and bankruptcy or insolvency, but mainly computer fraud and various harmful and abusive administration, looking at the new necessities arising in commerce with new technologies and looking at the responsibility that must be demanded from persons who manage the goods or patrimony of another person and do not act correctly in that administration;
- uuu) In modern and democratic society, justice and the exercise of justice are also goods that must obtain penal protection; thus punishment must be consecrated for falsity in procedural acts, for various obstructions to jurisdictional activity, non-exercise of jurisdictional activity when it constitutes denial of justice and violation of the secrecy of justice;
- vvv) Also include incrimination for acts constituting bribery, prevarication of magistrate or official and lawyer or public defender, and also other activity regarding personal favouring in justice, together with classic incriminations such as slanderous denunciation, simulation of crime or non-participation;
- www) Together with the references written in the last two numbers, conduct must also be criminalized that, in the exercise of public office, constitutes corruption, embezzlement, abuse of power or public force or economic participation in business by persons exercising public office or function;
- xxx) In an autonomous title, conduct constituting falsification of documents, technical notation, currency and stamped or sealed values, weights and measures, marks, stamps and cancellations must be prevented with adequate incriminating norms, with their various sanctions according to the nature of the falsified object, probative or fiduciary value and public use or destination, and provide for the possibility of seizure and forfeiture of objects destined to practice these crimes;
- yyy) In the area of economy, acts constituting tax fraud or smuggling and customs evasion regarding customs or border issues shall also be incriminated, together with criminal punishment for disobedience to requisitions ordered by the government and conduct that may disturb, prejudice or impede the realization of any public act, such as public tender or judicial auction.Article 3

Articulation with the Code of Criminal Procedure

The Criminal Code to which this legislative authorization refers must be articulated at the moment of its entry into force with the Code of Criminal Procedure and regulatory legislation on criminal matters that enters into force soon, in order to guarantee legal security and coherence in the respective legal system.

Article 4

Duration and extent

The legislative authorization granted by this law enters into force for 180 days, beginning from the moment this law enters into force.

Article 5

Entry into force

This legal diploma enters into force on the day following its publication.

Approved on 23 September 2008

President of the National Parliament in exercise

Maria da Paixão de Jesus da Costa

Promulgated on 7 October 2008

To be published.

President of the Republic

Dr. José Ramos Horta

Law 6/2009, of 15 July

(First amendment, as parliamentary review, to the Penal Code approved by Decree-Law 19/2009, of 8 April)

Life must be protected from the very moment of conception of a human being. Having accepted this principle, we cannot forget that there are also situations that may justify the interruption of pregnancy. However, because the goods that come into conflict here are extremely weighty, the good of the pregnant woman's life and the good of the foetus or embryo's life, both with equal value, it is only in extreme situations that we can consider this interruption legitimate: when with the sacrifice of one life it is possible to save the other life and it is not possible to save both lives at once. Thus, it must be established that interruption of pregnancy may only occur when with that interruption the pregnant woman will not die, and it is the woman who shall decide, according to her conscience, regarding that interruption.

Thus, pursuant to articles 92, no. 1, and 98 of the Constitution, the National Parliament decrees, to have the force of law, the following:

Article 1

Amendment to the Penal Code

Article 141 of the Penal Code approved by Decree-Law 19/2009, of 8 April, and annexed to that diploma, is amended as follows:

"Article 141

4. The provision of the previous number does not apply when, according to existing medical knowledge and experience and having done everything possible to save the pregnant woman and foetus or embryo's life, interruption of pregnancy is what can prevent the death of the pregnant woman, namely with medical certification and performed by a doctor or other health professional, under a doctor's direction, in a public or officially recognised health establishment, and with the pregnant woman's consent.

5. Before performing the interruption a panel of three doctors must certify the circumstances referred to in the previous number, in a written and signed medical certificate; in that panel participates one doctor who performs or supervises the performance of the interruption of pregnancy.

6. Consent of the pregnant woman shall be given personally in a written and signed document, having heard, when possible, her spouse, or person who lives with her as if married, or another person, according to the woman's request, who gives it, and, when possible, shall be given two days before the interruption is to be performed.

7. When the pregnant woman is a minor, her legal representative shall give consent.

8. When the pregnant woman is an adult or emancipated person who is psychologically incapacitated or a person who suffers from psychological incapacity, her spouse or person who lives with her as if married, or her legal representative, ascendant or descendant, or, in the absence thereof, any of her collateral relatives, respectively and successively, shall give this consent.

9. When it is not possible to obtain the medical certificate referred to in no. 5 and/or it is not possible to obtain consent as written in nos. 6 to 8, and the interruption must be performed quickly and can no longer wait, the doctor, having regard to each situation, shall decide, also using, when possible, the opinion of other doctors.

10. In the panel referred to in no. 5 shall participate a doctor who has adequate knowledge to evaluate the circumstances that justify the interruption of pregnancy, and, when possible, a gynaecologist/obstetrician.

11. The doctors who issue the medical certificate and the doctors who perform or supervise the performance of the interruption of pregnancy must provide the necessary explanations to the pregnant woman and, when applicable, to the persons referred to in nos. 7 and 8, namely regarding the method to be used in the interruption, the interruption's own effects and its consequences for the pregnant woman's physical and psychological health.

12. Doctors and other health professionals have the right to present conscientious objection to any act regarding interruption of pregnancy.

13. Doctors and other health professionals who invoke conscientious objection must guarantee the immediate intervention of other health professionals in the acts that must be performed and also immediate accompaniment of the pregnant woman.

14. Conscientious objection must be expressed explicitly and must be communicated to the clinical head at the health establishment where the objector works".

Article 2

Entry into force

This diploma enters into force on the day following its publication.

Approved on 26 May 2009

President of the National Parliament

Fernando La Sama de Araújo

Promulgated on 3 July 2009

To be published

President of the Republic

José Ramos Horta
